    function persist(notify=true){if(sessionReloading||sessionLoadFailed)return false;if(coop.active||coopPending){if(notify)toast('Online expeditions save automatically on the server');return false;}if(labActive){if(notify)toast('Campaign saves are locked while the isolated lab is open');return false;}if(!ensureSessionRevision())return false;try{storeSession(localStorage,state,true,persistedSessionRevision);saveStatus='Saved '+new Date().toLocaleTimeString();if(notify)toast('World saved on this device');return true;}catch(error){const checkpointBlocked=error instanceof Error&&error.name==='RollbackCheckpointError';saveStatus=checkpointBlocked?'Save paused · invalid rollback checkpoint':'Save failed';toast(checkpointBlocked?'A rollback checkpoint is invalid.     /** Changing foundations reloads the page: all render resources, physics workers, inputs,
     * cell catalogs, timers and UI are reconstructed together, never mixed across seeds. */
    function switchSession(next:State,replaceImported=false){if(sessionReloading||sessionLoadFailed)return;if(coop.active||coopPending||coop.status==='connecting'){toast('Return to solo before opening another local world');return;}if(labActive)return;if(sameWorld(state,next)){closePanel();toast('This world is already open. Your current progress is unchanged.');return;}if(!persist(false))return;try{const targetRevision=sessionResetRevision(localStorage,next),target=replaceImported?next:loadSavedWorld(localStorage,next)??next;storeSession(localStorage,target,true,targetRevision);reloadCommittedSession();}catch{toast('Could not open that world. Your current save is retained.');}}
    
    /** Reload is a single commit boundary. A cached outgoing page never resumes old writes. */
    function reloadCommittedSession(){
     if(sessionReloading)return;sessionReloading=true;clearInput();coopPanelDispose?.();coopPanelDispose=null;coop.disconnect();transitioning=true;physicsReady=false;zoneEpoch++;
     generationStartupController.abort();generationPanelDispose?.();generationPanelDispose=null;mapPanelDispose?.();mapPanelDispose=null;disposeGeneration();regionalStreamer?.dispose();
     regionalCalibrationController?.abort();wildernessProbeController?.abort();worker.onmessage=null;worker.terminate();renderer?.setAnimationLoop(null);gameAudio.suspend();
     panel.innerHTML='<h2>Opening your world…</h2><p>Your save is ready. The complete world is restarting together.</p><button id="reload-ready">Reload now</button>';$('reload-ready').onclick=()=>location.reload();
     location.reload();
    }

    function renderRestartPanel(kind:'restart'|'recover'){
     if(!ensureSessionRevision())return;
     const identity={generation:state.generation,seed:state.seed,...(state.regional?{regional:state.regional}:{})},epoch=zoneEpoch,recover=kind==='recover';
     const valid=()=>!sessionReloading&&!sessionLoadFailed&&!labActive&&!coop.active&&!coopPending&&coop.status!=='connecting'&&!transitioning&&physicsReady&&!panel.hidden&&panel.dataset.type===kind&&sameWorld(state,identity)&&zoneEpoch===epoch;

     try{historySaves=sessionRecoverySaves(localStorage,state);original=preResetSave(localStorage,state);selectedKey=historySaves.find(row=>row.raw===original)?.key??historySaves[0]?.key;operation=createSessionResetOperation(localStorage,state,recover?'restore':'restart',recover?selectedKey:undefined);}catch{toast('No valid recovery is available, or storage has changed.      panel.innerHTML=`<button class="close" aria-label="Cancel and close">×</button><span class="eyebrow">Personal solo save · seed ${state.seed}</span><h2>${recover?'Recover earlier progress?':'Restart this world?'}</h2><p class="restart-warning">${recover?'The selected recovery becomes this world’s active progress. This run is also kept in recovery history, so you can recover either later.':'Your current progress is kept as a protected recovery copy, separate from rolling autosaves.'} Other seeds, valley/frontier saves, settings and shared online rooms are unchanged.</p><p>The full game reloads after confirmation. To explore another seed, cancel and choose a frontier instead.</p><div class="row"><button id="reset-cancel">Cancel · keep playing</button><button id="reset-confirm" class="danger-confirm">${recover?'Recover & reload':'Restart & reload'}</button></div><p id="reset-error" role="status"></p>`;
     panel.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('.close')!.onclick=closePanel;$('reset-cancel').onclick=closePanel;
     const button=$('reset-confirm') as HTMLButtonElement;button.disabled=!valid()||recover&&!selectedKey;
     if(recover){const select=$('reset-checkpoint') as HTMLSelectElement;for(const row of historySaves){const option=document.createElement('option');option.value=row.key;option.textContent=`Before ${row.kind==='restart'?'restart':'recovery'} #${row.epoch}${row.raw===original?' · original protected run':''}`;select.append(option);}select.value=selectedKey??'';
      const preview=()=>{const row=historySaves.find(item=>item.key===select.value),saved=row?parseSave(row.raw):null;$('reset-preview').textContent=saved?`${saved.zone==='valley'?'Surface':saved.zone==='cave'?'River cave':'Echo Vault'} · ${saved.collected.length} collected caches · ${saved.inventory.scrap} scrap · ${saved.inventory.core} cores · ${saved.jobs.renown} renown · ${Math.round(saved.player.hp)}% suit`:'Recovery unavailable';};preview();
      select.onchange=()=>{if(!valid())return;try{selectedKey=select.value;operation=createSessionResetOperation(localStorage,state,'restore',selectedKey);button.disabled=false;$('reset-error').textContent='';preview();}catch{button.disabled=true;$('reset-error').textContent='That recovery changed or is unavailable.       $('reset-export').onclick=()=>{const row=historySaves.find(item=>item.key===select.value);if(row)downloadSaveText(row.raw,`axiom-before-${row.kind}-${row.epoch}.json`);};
     }
     button.onclick=()=>{if(!ensureSessionRevision()||!valid()||button.disabled)return;button.disabled=true;let committed=false;try{committed=operation.commit().committed;}catch{$('reset-error').textContent='Could not commit the change.      ($('reset-cancel') as HTMLButtonElement).focus({preventScroll:true});
    }

    function generatedShape(shape:{id:string;center:{x:number;y:number;z:number};half:{x:number;y:number;z:number};material:string;solid:boolean},color:string,roofBounds?:{x:number;z:number;hx:number;hz:number}){

     // Water and road ribbons sample the same authoritative ground triangles.
     function ribbon(points:readonly {x:number;y:number;z:number}[],width:number,material:THREE.Material,offset=0){const verts:number[]=[],ids:number[]=[];for(let i=0;i<points.length;i++){const p=points[i]!,a=points[Math.max(0,i-1)]!,b=points[Math.min(points.length-1,i+1)]!,len=Math.hypot(b.x-a.x,b.z-a.z)||1,nx=-(b.z-a.z)/len,nz=(b.x-a.x)/len;for(const side of [-1,1]){const x=p.x+nx*width/2*side,z=p.z+nz*width/2*side;verts.push(x,material===waterMat?p.y+offset:worldHeight({...state,zone:'valley'},x,z)+offset,z);}if(i){const j=i*2;ids.push(j-2,j-1,j,j-1,j+1,j);}}const geo=new THREE.BufferGeometry();geo.setAttribute('position',new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(verts,3));geo.setIndex(ids);geo.computeVertexNormals();const mesh=new THREE.Mesh(geo,material);mesh.receiveShadow=true;scene.add(mesh);}
     ribbon(valleyPlan.river.points,valleyPlan.river.width,waterMat,.03);
     for(const road of valleyPlan.roads)ribbon(road.points,road.width,mat('#b8b08a'),.025);

    const frontierTarget=new THREE.Vector3();
    function syncFrontierVisuals(dt:number,snap=false){
     const causal=state.causal;frontierRoot.visible=!!causal;if(!causal)return;
     for(const agent of causal.agents){
      const visual=residentVisuals.get(agent.id);if(!visual)continue;
      frontierTarget.set(agent.position.x,agent.position.y,agent.position.z);
      const oldX=visual.root.position.x,oldZ=visual.root.position.z;
      if(snap)visual.root.position.copy(frontierTarget);else visual.root.position.lerp(frontierTarget,1-Math.exp(-dt*18));
      const dx=visual.root.position.x-oldX,dz=visual.root.position.z-oldZ,distance=snap?0:Math.hypot(dx,dz);
      if(distance>.0001){const heading=Math.atan2(dx,dz),turn=Math.atan2(Math.sin(heading-visual.root.rotation.y),Math.cos(heading-visual.root.rotation.y));visual.root.rotation.y+=turn*(1-Math.exp(-dt*12));}
      visual.phase+=distance*4.5;
      const moving=dt>0?Math.min(1,distance/dt):0,stride=Math.sin(visual.phase)*.5*moving;
      visual.leftLeg.rotation.x=stride;visual.rightLeg.rotation.x=-stride;visual.leftArm.rotation.x=-stride*.65;visual.rightArm.rotation.x=agent.cargo>0?-.35:stride*.65;
      visual.cargo.visible=agent.cargo>0;visual.root.userData.status=agent.status;
     }
     for(const workplace of causal.workplaces){const visual=workplaceVisuals.get(workplace.id);if(!visual)continue;const color=workplace.operational?'#91e3bd':'#e2a36f';visual.beacon.material=mat(color);visual.ring.material=mat(color);visual.tool.rotation.z=workplace.operational?0:-.5;visual.beacon.rotation.y+=dt*(workplace.operational?.8:.15);}
     if(sourceVisual){sourceVisual.beacon.material=mat(causal.sourceRepaired?'#91e3bd':causal.depot>0?'#7cccd7':'#e2a36f');sourceVisual.water.visible=causal.depot>0;sourceVisual.beacon.rotation.y+=dt*.5;}
    }

    const pendingCells=new Map<string,{epoch:number;revision:number;group:THREE.Group;obstacles:Obstacle[]}>();

    function disposeCellGroup(group:THREE.Group){group.removeFromParent();for(const feature of wildernessCatalog){const root=group.getObjectByName(feature.id);if(root)wildernessView.forget(feature.id,root);}for(const o of valleyObjects){const visual=visuals.get(o.id);if(!visual||!group.getObjectById(visual.id))continue;visuals.delete(o.id);const creature=creatureVisuals.get(o.id);if(creature)creatureVisuals.delete(o.id);}const owned:THREE.Object3D[]=[];group.traverse(o=>{if(typeof o.userData.disposeOwned==='function')owned.push(o);});for(const root of owned){root.userData.disposeOwned();delete root.userData.disposeOwned;root.removeFromParent();}group.traverse(o=>{if(o instanceof THREE.Mesh){o.geometry.dispose();const ms=Array.isArray(o.material)?o.material:[o.material];for(const m of ms)if(m instanceof THREE.MeshBasicMaterial)m.dispose();}});}
    function disposeCell(key:string){const group=residentGroups.get(key);if(group)disposeCellGroup(group);residentGroups.delete(key);const pending=pendingCells.get(key);if(pending)disposeCellGroup(pending.group);pendingCells.delete(key);confirmedCells.delete(key);cellObstacles.delete(key);}
    function stageCell(key:string,revision:number){
     const old=pendingCells.get(key);if(old)disposeCellGroup(old.group);
     const group=new THREE.Group();group.name=`cell:${key}`;
     for(const create of cellFactories.get(key)??[])group.add(create());
     const physical:Obstacle[]=[...(state.regional?staticCellObstacles.get(key)??[]:[])];
     for(const feature of cellFeatures.get(key)??[]){if(!wildernessFeatureRemaining(feature,state.wilderness))continue;group.add(wildernessView.create(feature,wildernessFeatureDepleted(feature,state.wilderness)));physical.push(...feature.solids);}
     for(const object of objects){if(ownerOf(object)!==key)continue;const visual=visuals.get(object.id);if(visual)visual.visible=!state.collected.includes(object.id)&&!state.defeated.includes(object.id);}
     pendingCells.set(key,{epoch:zoneEpoch,revision,group,obstacles:physical});return {key,revision,obstacles:physical};
    }
    function confirmCell(key:string,revision:number){const pending=pendingCells.get(key);if(!pending||pending.epoch!==zoneEpoch||pending.revision!==revision)return false;const old=residentGroups.get(key);if(old)disposeCellGroup(old);pendingCells.delete(key);residentGroups.set(key,pending.group);valleyRoot.add(pending.group);cellObstacles.set(key,pending.obstacles);confirmedCells.set(key,revision);return true;}

    function streamWorld(){if(labActive||!physicsReady||state.zone!=='valley')return;if(state.regional)regionalStreamer?.update(targetPosition.x,targetPosition.z);const delta=streamer.update(state.player.x,state.player.z,state);
     for(const c of delta.unloaded){disposeCell(c.key);sendPhysics({type:'cell-unload',key:c.key,revision:c.revision});}
     for(const c of [...delta.loaded,...delta.changed])sendPhysics({type:'cell-load',...stageCell(c.key,c.revision)});
    }

    const regionalConfirmedTrade=new Map<string,string>();
    const regionalFoodView=state.regional?createRegionalFoodView(state.seed):null;if(regionalFoodView)valleyRoot.add(regionalFoodView.root);
    const regionalLandscape=state.regional?createRegionalLandscape(state.seed):null;const scenicWater=createScenicWater();valleyRoot.add(scenicWater.root);if(regionalLandscape)valleyRoot.add(regionalLandscape.root);
    const regionalPending=new Map<string,{entry:RegionalEntry;group:THREE.Group;obstacles:Obstacle[];projects:{id:string;started:boolean}[];tradeTargets:{id:string;signature:string}[]}>();
    const regionalConfirmedObstacles=new Map<string,Obstacle[]>();let regionalPhysicsStats:unknown=null;let regionalLoadTarget:{x:number;z:number}|null=null;
    const regionalTravel={metres:0,seconds:0,last:null as {x:number;z:number;step:number;epoch:number}|null,speed:0};
    let regionalCalibration:RegionalCalibrationReport|null=null,regionalCalibrationRunning=false;let regionalCalibrationController:AbortController|null=null;
    function regionalPhysical(entry:RegionalEntry){const tradeBoxes=state.frontierTrade?regionalTradeProjectBoxes(state.seed,state.frontierTrade).filter(b=>`${Math.floor(b.center.x/64)}:${Math.floor(b.center.z/64)}`===entry.chunk.key):[];const supplyBoxes=state.frontierSupply?regionalSupplyProjectBoxes(state.seed,state.frontierSupply).filter(b=>regionalSupplyPlan(state.seed).outposts.some(o=>o.ownerChunk===entry.chunk.key&&b.id.startsWith(o.id+'/'))):[];return [...tradeBoxes.filter(b=>b.solid).map(b=>({featureId:b.id,x:b.center.x,y:b.center.y,z:b.center.z,hx:b.half.x,hy:b.half.y,hz:b.half.z})),...supplyBoxes.filter(b=>b.solid).map(b=>({featureId:b.id,x:b.center.x,y:b.center.y,z:b.center.z,hx:b.half.x,hy:b.half.y,hz:b.half.z})),...entry.chunk.structures.filter(s=>s.solid).map(s=>({x:s.center.x,y:s.center.y,z:s.center.z,hx:s.half.x,hy:s.half.y,hz:s.half.z})),...entry.chunk.features.filter(f=>wildernessFeatureRemaining(f,state.wilderness)).flatMap(f=>f.solids)];}
    function regionalPacket(entry:RegionalEntry){const pending=regionalPending.get(entry.key)!;return {key:entry.key,revision:entry.revision,obstacles:pending.obstacles,terrain:entry.chunk.terrain,bounds:entry.chunk.bounds};}
    function stageRegionalCell(entry:RegionalEntry,initial:boolean){if(!regionalView)return;const old=regionalPending.get(entry.key);if(old)regionalView.discard(old.group);const group=regionalView.make(entry.chunk,state.wilderness);for(const site of entry.chunk.sites)makeLabel(site.name.toUpperCase(),site.position.x,site.position.z,group);regionalPending.set(entry.key,{entry,group,obstacles:regionalPhysical(entry),tradeTargets:state.frontierTrade?regionalTradeChunkTargets(entry.chunk.key).map(id=>({id,signature:regionalTradeTargetSignature(state.seed,state.frontierTrade,id)})):[],projects:regionalSupplyPlan(state.seed).outposts.filter(o=>o.ownerChunk===entry.chunk.key).map(o=>({id:o.id,started:state.frontierSupply?.outposts.find(p=>p.id===o.id)?.buildStartedAt!=null}))});if(!initial)sendPhysics({type:'cell-load',...regionalPacket(entry)});}
    const regionalStreamer=state.regional?new RegionalStreamer(state.seed,createRegionalGenerator(),stageRegionalCell,(key,revision)=>sendPhysics({type:'cell-unload',key,revision}),()=>`${state.wilderness?.harvested.length??0}:${regionalSupplyProjectSignature(state.frontierSupply)}:${regionalTradeProjectSignature(state.frontierTrade)}`):null;
    function localWildernessCatalog(){return [...wildernessCatalog,...[...(regionalStreamer?.entries.values()??[])].filter(e=>e.confirmed).flatMap(e=>e.chunk.features)];}
    function clearRegionalProjection(){regionalStreamer?.reset();regionalView?.clear();for(const p of regionalPending.values())regionalView?.discard(p.group);regionalPending.clear();regionalConfirmedObstacles.clear();regionalConfirmedProjects.clear();regionalSupplyView?.clear();regionalConfirmedTrade.clear();regionalTradeView?.clear();regionalFoodView?.clear();regionalTravel.last=null;}
    function confirmRegionalCell(key:string,revision:number,loaded:boolean){if(!regionalStreamer?.acknowledge(key,revision,loaded))return;const pending=regionalPending.get(key);if(loaded){if(pending?.entry.revision===revision){regionalView!.commit(key,pending.group);regionalConfirmedObstacles.set(key,pending.obstacles);for(const p of pending.projects)regionalConfirmedProjects.set(p.id,p.started);for(const p of pending.tradeTargets)regionalConfirmedTrade.set(p.id,p.signature);regionalPending.delete(key);}}else{if(pending){regionalView!.discard(pending.group);regionalPending.delete(key);}regionalView?.unload(key);regionalConfirmedObstacles.delete(key);if(state.frontierTrade)for(const id of regionalTradeChunkTargets(key.replace(/^region:/,'')))regionalConfirmedTrade.delete(id);for(const o of regionalSupplyPlan(state.seed).outposts)if('region:'+o.ownerChunk===key)regionalConfirmedProjects.delete(o.id);}}
    function rejectRegionalCell(key:string,revision:number,loaded:boolean,reason:string){if(!regionalStreamer?.reject(key,revision,loaded))return;const pending=regionalPending.get(key);if(pending){regionalView?.discard(pending.group);regionalPending.delete(key);}console.warn('Regional collision packet rejected',reason);}
    async function sendWorldPacket(type:'init'|'zone',extra:Record<string,unknown>={}){
     const epoch=zoneEpoch;clearRegionalProjection();const region=state.regional&&state.zone==='valley';regionalLoadTarget=region?{x:state.player.x,z:state.player.z}:null;
     try{if(region){await regionalStreamer!.initial(state.player.x,state.player.z);if(epoch!==zoneEpoch||labActive)return;}
      const base=initialCellPacket(),initialCells=[...(base.initialCells??[]),...[...regionalPending.values()].map(p=>regionalPacket(p.entry))];
      sendPhysics({type,x:state.player.x,z:state.player.z,obstacles:zoneObstacles(),...contactWorldPacket(),initialCells,bound:worldBound(state),y:contactSpawnY(),...(region?{streamedTerrain:true}:state.zone==='valley'&&valleyPlan?{terrain:valleyPlan.terrain}:{}),...extra});
     }catch(error){if(epoch!==zoneEpoch||(error as Error).name==='AbortError')return;physicsReady=false;transitioning=true;rejectPhysics(error instanceof Error?error:Error('Regional generation failed'));toast('World generation stopped safely. Reload your saved world to retry.');}
    }
    addEventListener('pagehide',(event:PageTransitionEvent)=>{if(!event.persisted){regionalStreamer?.dispose();regionalLandscape?.dispose();regionalSupplyView?.dispose();regionalTradeView?.dispose();regionalFoodView?.dispose();scenicWater.dispose();}regionalCalibrationController?.abort();});
    function recordRegionalTravel(m:{x:number;z:number;step:number}){if(!state.regional||state.zone!=='valley'||labActive)return;const old=regionalTravel.last;regionalTravel.last={x:m.x,z:m.z,step:m.step,epoch:zoneEpoch};if(!old||old.epoch!==zoneEpoch||m.step<=old.step)return;const distance=Math.hypot(m.x-old.x,m.z-old.z),seconds=(m.step-old.step)/60;if(distance>3||seconds>1)return;regionalTravel.speed=distance/seconds;if(panel.hidden&&!document.hidden&&windowActive){regionalTravel.metres+=distance;regionalTravel.seconds+=seconds;}}
    function regionalDiagnostics(){return state.regional?{version:1,seed:state.seed,areaSquareMetres:REGION_AREA,sideMetres:REGION_BOUND*2,chunkMetres:CHUNK_SIZE,metresPerUnit:1,stream:regionalStreamer?.stats??null,physics:regionalPhysicsStats,renderChunks:regionalView?.count??0,renderFeatures:regionalView?.featureCount??0,rendererMemory:renderer?{...renderer.info.memory}:null,memoryScope:'Renderer-tracked allocations when available, not total device RAM or a certified GPU memory benchmark',travel:{metres:regionalTravel.metres,simulationSeconds:regionalTravel.seconds,metresPerSecond:regionalTravel.speed},calibration:regionalCalibration,landscape:regionalLandscape?.stats??null,visibility:{zoom,fog:zoomAtmosphere(zoom,true,state.zone!=='valley',labActive),far:camera.far},scope:'Actual fixed-step travel; collision-confirmed local detail with render-only distant terrain. No physical-device performance certification.'}:null;}
    function drawRegionalOverview(context:CanvasRenderingContext2D,scale:number){const plan=regionalPlan(state.seed);context.fillStyle='#56796b';context.fillRect(-REGION_BOUND*scale,-REGION_BOUND*scale,REGION_BOUND*2*scale,REGION_BOUND*2*scale);for(let z=-REGION_BOUND;z<REGION_BOUND;z+=80)for(let x=-REGION_BOUND;x<REGION_BOUND;x+=80){context.fillStyle=regionalBiomeAt(state.seed,x+40,z+40).color;context.fillRect(x*scale,z*scale,Math.min(80,REGION_BOUND-x)*scale+.3,Math.min(80,REGION_BOUND-z)*scale+.3);}const path=(points:readonly {x:number;z:number}[],color:string,width:number)=>{context.strokeStyle=color;context.lineWidth=width;context.beginPath();points.forEach((p,i)=>i?context.lineTo(p.x*scale,p.z*scale):context.moveTo(p.x*scale,p.z*scale));context.stroke();};for(const r of plan.water)path(r.points,'#7fc4c2',2);for(const r of plan.roads)path(r.points,'#ddc695',1);for(const site of plan.sites){context.fillStyle=site.kind==='outpost'?'#f1d493':site.kind==='entrance'?'#8de2d4':'#c5d4bb';context.fillRect(site.position.x*scale-2,site.position.z*scale-2,4,4);}context.strokeStyle='#d9e5cd';context.lineWidth=.7;context.strokeRect(-80*scale,-80*scale,160*scale,160*scale);}
    function mountRegionalLab(){const host=document.getElementById('regional-lab');if(!host)return;if(!state.regional){host.innerHTML='<p>This save keeps its original valley. Choose a frontier in Settings to open a separate 10 km² world without replacing it.</p>';return;}const stats=regionalStreamer!.stats,side=REGION_BOUND*2,plan=regionalPlan(state.seed),route=regionalRoute(state.seed,plan.sites[2]!.id,plan.sites[11]!.id);host.innerHTML=`<p>10.000 km² · ${side.toFixed(3)} × ${side.toFixed(3)} m · 1 world unit = 1 metre. 64 m chunks, 2 m terrain samples. Regional world v1, seed ${state.seed}.</p><p>${stats.resident} resident / 49 maximum · ${stats.confirmed} collision-confirmed · ${stats.queued} queued · ${stats.inFlight} generating · ${stats.vertices.toLocaleString()} resident vertices · peak ${stats.peakResident} · ${stats.features} regional props / ${stats.structures} structure pieces (peaks ${stats.peakFeatures}/${stats.peakStructures}). Generation: ${stats.backend}. Renderer-tracked memory: ${renderer?.info.memory.total?((renderer.info.memory.total/1048576).toFixed(1)+' MiB'):'not reported'}; this is not total device RAM.</p><p>Travel since this session: ${regionalTravel.metres.toFixed(1)} m in ${regionalTravel.seconds.toFixed(1)} active simulation seconds. Last measured speed ${regionalTravel.speed.toFixed(2)} m/s. Menus and teleport jumps are excluded.</p><p>Model targets: walk 2 m/s (36% thumb), jog 4 m/s (58%), keyboard run 5 m/s, sprint 8 m/s. Straight edge-to-edge lower bounds: ${(side/2/60).toFixed(2)} / ${(side/4/60).toFixed(2)} / ${(side/5/60).toFixed(2)} / ${(side/8/60).toFixed(2)} minutes respectively, plus acceleration. These are horizontal straight-line estimates. Slopes, trail distance, obstacles, pauses and loading guards add time. Sprint currently has no locomotion stamina drain; staff stamina is separate.</p><p>Corner-to-corner diagonal: ${(side*Math.SQRT2).toFixed(3)} m. ${route?`${plan.sites[2]!.name} → ${plan.sites[11]!.name}: ${(route.horizontalMetres/1000).toFixed(3)} km horizontal trail / ${(route.surfaceMetres/1000).toFixed(3)} km ground-following, maximum sampled grade ${(route.maxGrade*100).toFixed(1)}%. At steady 5 m/s this is ${(route.horizontalMetres/5/60).toFixed(2)} minutes before steering, grade or obstruction delays.`:''}</p><p>The complete trail network is ${(plan.roads.reduce((n,r)=>n+r.points.slice(1).reduce((m,p,i)=>m+Math.hypot(p.x-r.points[i]!.x,p.z-r.points[i]!.z,p.y-r.points[i]!.y),0),0)/1000).toFixed(2)} km; it is not a single edge-to-edge route.</p><button id="regional-calibrate">${regionalCalibrationRunning?'Cancel calibration':'Measure actual walk / jog / run / sprint'}</button><p id="regional-calibration-status"></p><div class="row"><button id="regional-refresh">Refresh measurements</button><button id="regional-export">Export streaming report</button></div><p>${stats.error?'Generation issue: '+stats.error:'Detailed geometry loads before movement is allowed onto it. Distant terrain uses render-only scenery; zooming out clears the fog.'}</p><details><summary>Solo survey teleport · preloads collision</summary><p>Moves this player only; current progress is retained. Travel telemetry excludes the jump. Unavailable online or in the isolated lab.</p><select id="regional-destination"><option value="camp">Starting camp</option>${plan.sites.map((p,i)=>`<option value="${i}">${p.name}</option>`).join('')}</select><button id="regional-teleport" ${coop.active||coopPending||labActive||transitioning?'disabled':''}>Survey destination</button></details>`;
     const output=$('regional-calibration-status');if(regionalCalibration){output.textContent=regionalCalibration.status+': '+regionalCalibration.measurements.map(m=>`${m.gait} (${m.inputMode}) ${m.steadySpeed.toFixed(4)} m/s · 99% in ${m.accelerationSeconds.toFixed(3)} s · side crossing ${(side/m.steadySpeed/60).toFixed(2)} min steady flat estimate`).join(' | ')+(regionalCalibration.error?' · '+regionalCalibration.error:'');}else output.textContent='Run a separate disposable production-physics worker. Six ten-second fixed-step traces, including 5 m/s keyboard run and 6 m/s analog run. This does not move or save the campaign.';
     $('regional-calibrate').onclick=()=>{if(regionalCalibrationRunning){regionalCalibrationController?.abort();return;}regionalCalibrationRunning=true;const controller=new AbortController();regionalCalibrationController=controller;mountRegionalLab();void runRegionalCalibration({signal:controller.signal,progress:(done,total)=>{const status=document.getElementById('regional-calibration-status');if(status)status.textContent=`Measured ${done}/${total} controller inputs`;}}).then(report=>{regionalCalibration=report;regionalCalibrationRunning=false;regionalCalibrationController=null;if(!panel.hidden&&panel.dataset.type==='dev')mountRegionalLab();});};
     $('regional-refresh').onclick=()=>mountRegionalLab();$('regional-export').onclick=()=>downloadJSON(regionalDiagnostics(),'axiom-streamed-frontier-report.json');$('regional-teleport').onclick=()=>{if(coop.active||coopPending||labActive||transitioning)return;const value=($('regional-destination') as HTMLSelectElement).value,p=value==='camp'?endpoints.spawn:plan.sites[Number(value)]?.position;if(!p)return;const next=applyAction(state,{type:'move',x:p.x,z:p.z});restoreWorld(next);closePanel();toast('Preparing terrain and collision at the survey destination');};
    }
    const worker=new Worker(new URL('./physics.worker.ts',import.meta.url),{type:'module'});
    function sendPhysics(message:Record<string,unknown>){if(labRunning&&message.type==='input'&&!message.paused){const i=labInput();message={...message,x:i.x,z:i.z,crouch:!!i.crouch,jump:!!i.jump,analog:true};}if(message.type==='teleport'&&!labActive&&state.zone==='valley')streamWorld();worker.postMessage({...message,epoch:zoneEpoch});}
    function showZone(){
     const inside=state.zone!=='valley';valleyRoot.visible=!inside;dungeonRoot.visible=state.zone==='dungeon';caveRoot.visible=state.zone==='cave';caveFloodSignature='';caveView.sync(state.caveWater,state.collected,state.caveSupply);objects=encounterObjects(state);
     scene.background=new THREE.Color(inside?'#1c3039':'#a7c8b4');scene.fog=new THREE.Fog(inside?'#1c3039':'#a7c8b4',state.regional&&!inside?45:65,state.regional&&!inside?95:135);
     document.querySelector('.location strong')!.textContent=state.zone==='cave'?'RIVER CAVE':inside?'ECHO VAULT':'VERDANT REACH';document.querySelector('.map-label')!.textContent=state.zone==='cave'?'N ↑   RIVER CAVE':inside?'N ↑   ECHO VAULT':'N ↑   VERDANT REACH';
     $('minimap').setAttribute('aria-label',state.zone==='cave'?'Natural river cave map':inside?'Connected Echo Vault dungeon map':'Valley map');
     canvas.setAttribute('aria-label',state.zone==='cave'?'Interactive natural river cave with changing flood depths':inside?'Interactive connected cave dungeon':'Interactive three-dimensional river valley');
     for(const o of dungeon.objects){const g=dungeonVisuals.get(o.id)!;g.visible=!state.collected.includes(o.id)&&!state.defeated.includes(o.id);}
     targetPosition.set(state.player.x,contactSpawnY(),state.player.z);player.position.copy(targetPosition);cameraTarget.copy(targetPosition);cameraTarget.y+=1;
    }
    function restoreWorld(next:State,save=false){
     if(sessionReloading||sessionLoadFailed||labActive)return false;
     if(!sameWorld(state,next)){switchSession(next);return false;}
     clearInput();transitioning=true;physicsReady=false;zoneEpoch++;state=enableRegionalFood(enableRegionalTrade(enableRegionalSupply(enableWaterRequests(enableCommonsTrade(enableCaveSupply(enableEcology(enableEconomy(enableEncounters(next.player.hp<=0&&!coop.active?applyAction(next,{type:'respawn'}):next)))))))));pulseTime=0;pulse.visible=false;animation=idleAnimation();physicsMotion={vx:0,vz:0,vy:0,landing:0,landingSpeed:0,grounded:true,crouched:false,stance:0,sliding:false};playerContact=emptyPlayerContact();movableBodies=[];contactRequestId=null;avatarAction=null;combo=retireComboCommitment(combo);cancelGuardInput('zone');enemyHP.clear();
     for(const key of new Set([...residentGroups.keys(),...pendingCells.keys()]))disposeCell(key);streamer=new CellStreamer(streamObjects,cellPositions);
     machineSignature='';showZone();syncFrontierVisuals(0,true);if(state.regional)void sendWorldPacket('zone');else sendPhysics({type:'zone',x:state.player.x,z:state.player.z,obstacles:zoneObstacles(),...contactWorldPacket(),...initialCellPacket(),bound:worldBound(state),y:contactSpawnY(),...(state.zone==='valley'&&valleyPlan?{terrain:valleyPlan.terrain}:{})});sync();return !save||persist(false);
    }
    showZone();void sendWorldPacket('init');
    worker.onmessage=(e)=>{const m=e.data;if(m.type==='zone-rejected'&&m.requestedEpoch===zoneEpoch){physicsReady=false;transitioning=true;failLabRestore('World replacement rejected safely');rejectPhysics(Error(m.reason??'World replacement rejected'));toast('World loading stopped safely. Your stored saves are retained; reload to retry.');return;}if(m.epoch!==zoneEpoch)return;
     if(m.type==='cell-ack'){if(String(m.key).startsWith('region:')){confirmRegionalCell(m.key,m.revision,m.loaded);}else if(m.loaded)confirmCell(m.key,m.revision);return;}
     if(m.type==='cell-rejected'&&String(m.key).startsWith('region:')){rejectRegionalCell(m.key,m.revision,m.loaded===true,m.reason);return;}
     if(m.type==='online-motion-ack'){lastCoopMotionAck={revision:m.revision,applied:m.applied,reason:m.reason??null};return;}
     if(m.type==='captured'){if(pendingLabRestore&&m.requestId===pendingLabRestore.id){clearTimeout(restoreTimer);const pass=matchingPose(m.checkpoint,pendingLabRestore.physics);if(labReport)labReport.model.push({name:'Acknowledged physics pose restoration',pass,actual:pass?'Worker capture matches original position, height, momentum and stance':'Worker capture differs from original pose',expected:'Exact original airborne/crouched pose within 0.00001 m tolerance'});pendingLabRestore=null;transitioning=false;if(pass)toast('Original campaign pose verified and restored');if(!pass)toast('Campaign data is restored, but its physics pose did not match. Check the lab report.');}else if(m.requestId===labCaptureToken)beginLab(m.checkpoint);return;}
     if(m.type==='error'){failLabRestore('Physics worker reported an error');transitioning=true;physicsReady=false;interruptLab('Physics failed');rejectPhysics(new Error(m.message));toast('Movement stopped. Reload the saved world to retry.');}
     else if(m.type==='ready'){if(state.regional)regionalLoadTarget=null;lastPhysicsStep=m.step??-1;physicsReady=true;transitioning=false;for(const [key,pending]of pendingCells)confirmCell(key,pending.revision);if(state.regional){for(const [key,pending]of regionalPending)confirmRegionalCell(key,pending.entry.revision,true);regionalStreamer?.initialReady();}machineSignature='';if(labActive){sendPhysics({type:'tuning',value:labTuning});if(labRunning)sendPhysics({type:'lab-script',id:LAB_CASES[labCaseIndex]!.id});labWarmup=60;sendMovement();}else{syncMachine();streamWorld();syncCavePhysics();const snapshot=coop.snapshot;if(coop.active&&snapshot?.motion)sendPhysics({type:'online-motion',revision:snapshot.revision,x:snapshot.world.player.x,z:snapshot.world.player.z,motion:snapshot.motion});}if(pendingLabRestore){transitioning=true;sendPhysics({type:'capture',requestId:pendingLabRestore.id});}resolvePhysics();updateBackend();}
     else if(m.type==='snapshot'&&!transitioning){
      if(state.regional){regionalPhysicsStats=m.streaming??null;recordRegionalTravel(m);}
      if(contactRequestId!==null&&m.contactRequestId===contactRequestId)contactRequestId=null;
      playerContact=validPlayerContact(m.contact)?m.contact:emptyPlayerContact();if(validMovableBodies(m.movable))movableBodies=m.movable;
      if(contactEnabled()&&state.zone==='valley'&&state.generation===2){const traversal=traversalFromBodies(movableBodies,state);if(traversal&&JSON.stringify(traversal)!==JSON.stringify(state.traversal)){state={...state,traversal};}}
      targetPosition.set(m.x,m.feetY??Math.max(0,m.y-1.09),m.z);physicsMotion={vx:m.vx??0,vz:m.vz??0,vy:m.vy??0,landing:m.landing??0,landingSpeed:m.landingSpeed??0,grounded:m.grounded,crouched:m.crouched===true,stance:m.stance??0,sliding:m.sliding===true};
      const simulated=m.step!==lastPhysicsStep;lastPhysicsStep=m.step;const playing=simulated&&panel.hidden&&!document.hidden&&windowActive;if(playing&&combo.phase==='idle'&&!guardBusy(guard))combatHeading=stepFacing(combatHeading,physicsMotion.vx,physicsMotion.vz,1/60,labActive?labTuning.turnRate:DEFAULT_TUNING.turnRate);
      if(labActive){if(playing&&m.step!==lastLabStep){lastLabStep=m.step;tickLab(m);}}
      else if(coop.active||coopPending){if(playing&&coop.canAct){coop.move({x:m.x,z:m.z,y:targetPosition.y,facing:combatHeading,grounded:physicsMotion.grounded,crouched:physicsMotion.crouched});stepCombat();}}
      else{state=applyAction(state,{type:'move',x:m.x,z:m.z});if(playing){objects=encounterObjects(state);const encounter=stepGuardedWorldEncounters(state,guard,guardContext());guard=encounter.guard;consumeGuardEvents(encounter.guardEvents);state=encounter.state;objects=encounterObjects(state);for(const event of encounter.events){if(event.type==='prepare')gameAudio.cue('enemy-telegraph',{distance:Math.hypot((state.encounters?.enemies.find(e=>e.id===event.enemyId)?.x??1000)-state.player.x,(state.encounters?.enemies.find(e=>e.id===event.enemyId)?.z??1000)-state.player.z)});if(event.type==='damage'){sendPhysics({type:'contact-release'});gameAudio.cue('hurt');consumeCombat(cancelCombo(combo,'stun',combatTuning(),SENTRY_HIT_STUN_SECONDS));impactAge=0;impactStrength=.45;}}stepCombat();state=applyAction(state,{type:'tick',dt:1/60});syncCavePhysics();}$('hp').style.width=state.player.hp+'%';if(state.player.hp<=0){restoreWorld(applyAction(state,{type:'respawn'}),true);toast('Your field suit returned you to camp. Supplies retained.');}}
     }
    };
    worker.onerror=()=>{failLabRestore('Physics worker stopped');physicsReady=false;transitioning=true;rejectPhysics(new Error('Physics initialization failed'));toast('Physics stopped. Reload to recover your last save.');};
    function updateBackend(){$('backend').textContent=`${backend.toUpperCase()} · RAPIER ${physicsReady?'0.20':'LOADING'} · SEED ${state.seed}`;}
    const keys=new Set<string>();let windowActive=document.hasFocus();coop.setSuspended(!windowActive||document.hidden);
    const pointers=new GameplayPointers();const stick=pointers.stick;let crouch=false,jump=false,heldCrouch=false,heldJump=false;
    const coarseQuery=matchMedia('(any-pointer: coarse)');
    let touchUI=hasTouchControls(navigator.maxTouchPoints,coarseQuery.matches);
    let inputMode:'touch'|'desktop'=coarseQuery.matches?'touch':'desktop';
    let mobileAutoPulse=true;try{mobileAutoPulse=localStorage.getItem('axiom-touch-auto-pulse')!=='off';}catch{/* Keep the safe in-session default when storage is unavailable. */}
    const touchButtons=bindTouchButtons(app,()=>{touchUI=true;inputMode='touch';updateTouchUI();});
    app.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{if(e.pointerType==='mouse'||e.pointerType==='pen')inputMode='desktop';});
    function updateTouchUI(){document.documentElement.classList.toggle('touch-ui',touchUI);}
    updateTouchUI();coarseQuery.addEventListener('change',()=>{touchUI=hasTouchControls(navigator.maxTouchPoints,coarseQuery.matches);updateTouchUI();});
    const crouchHold=bindHoldButton($('crouch-btn'),value=>{heldCrouch=value;refreshHeldActions();});
    const jumpHold=bindHoldButton($('jump-btn'),value=>{heldJump=value;refreshHeldActions();});
    function refreshHeldActions(){
     crouch=heldCrouch||keys.has('KeyC');const requestedJump=heldJump||keys.has('Space');
     if(coop.active&&coop.canAct&&!coopPending&&coop.snapshot?.motion&&requestedJump&&!coopJumpHeld&&physicsReady&&!transitioning&&panel.hidden&&!document.hidden&&windowActive&&state.player.hp>0)coop.jump();
     coopJumpHeld=requestedJump;jump=!coop.active&&!coopPending&&requestedJump;
     $('crouch-btn').setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(crouch));$('jump-btn').setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(coop.active?requestedJump:jump));
     $('crouch-btn').querySelector('.action-title')!.textContent=crouch?'Crouching':'Crouch';
     sendMovement();
    }
    function sendMovement(){
     if(crouch||jump||coopJumpHeld)cancelGuardInput(crouch?'crouch':'jump');
     const blocked=!panel.hidden||transitioning||!physicsReady||document.hidden||!windowActive||coopPending||(coop.active&&!coop.canAct)||(labActive&&livePractice?.status==='paused'&&!liveResumeRequested);
     const liveDead=labActive&&livePractice?.status==='dead';
     const kx=(keys.has('KeyD')||keys.has('ArrowRight')?1:0)-(keys.has('KeyA')||keys.has('ArrowLeft')?1:0),kz=(keys.has('KeyS')||keys.has('ArrowDown')?1:0)-(keys.has('KeyW')||keys.has('ArrowUp')?1:0);
     const input=movementInput(kx,kz,stick.value.x,stick.value.z,orbit,keys.has('ShiftLeft')||keys.has('ShiftRight'));
     sendPhysics({type:'input',x:blocked||liveDead?0:input.x,z:blocked||liveDead?0:input.z,analog:true,crouch:!blocked&&!liveDead&&crouch,jump:!blocked&&!liveDead&&jump,paused:blocked});
    }
    function clearInput(){
     cancelGuardInput('menu');
     contactRequestId=null;
     const ids=pointers.pointerIds;pointers.reset();touchButtons.reset();
     keys.clear();heldCrouch=false;heldJump=false;crouchHold.reset();jumpHold.reset();refreshHeldActions();
     for(const id of ids)if(canvas.hasPointerCapture(id))canvas.releasePointerCapture(id);
     sendPhysics({type:'input',x:0,z:0,crouch:false,jump:false,sprint:false,paused:true});
    }

    function closePanel(updateHistory:unknown=true){if(sessionReloading)return;cancelLabEntry();generationPanelDispose?.();generationPanelDispose=null;mapPanelDispose?.();mapPanelDispose=null;coopPanelDispose?.();coopPanelDispose=null;equipmentPanelDispose?.();equipmentPanelDispose=null;panel.hidden=true;document.documentElement.classList.remove('panel-open');clearInput();if(labActive&&livePractice?.status==='paused'&&livePractice.pause==='menu')liveResumeRequested=true;const focus=panelReturnFocus?.isConnected?panelReturnFocus:$('settings');focus.focus({preventScroll:true});if(updateHistory!==false)panelNavigation.dismiss();}
    addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
     const target=e.target as HTMLElement;
     if(e.code==='Tab'&&!panel.hidden){const items=[...panel.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('button:not(:disabled),input:not([hidden]):not(:disabled),select:not(:disabled),summary,a[href],[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])')].filter(el=>el.getClientRects().length);const first=items[0],last=items.at(-1);if(first&&last&&((e.shiftKey&&(target===first||target===panel))||(!e.shiftKey&&(target===last||target===panel)))){e.preventDefault();(e.shiftKey?last:first).focus();}return;}
     const controlTarget=target.isContentEditable||!!target.closest('input,select,textarea,[contenteditable="true"],[contenteditable="plaintext-only"]')||(!!target.closest('button')&&(e.code==='Space'||e.code==='Enter'));
     const action=keyboardAction(e.code,controlTarget);
     if(action==='close'){e.preventDefault();closePanel();return;}
     if(controlTarget||e.metaKey||e.ctrlKey||e.altKey)return;
     inputMode='desktop';
     if(action==='map'){e.preventDefault();if(!e.repeat)openPanel('map');return;}
     if(!panel.hidden&&action!=='build'&&action!=='journal'&&action!=='save')return;
     if(['KeyW','KeyA','KeyS','KeyD','Space','ArrowUp','ArrowDown','ArrowLeft','ArrowRight'].includes(e.code))e.preventDefault();
     if(e.repeat)return;keys.add(e.code);
     if((action==='crouch'||action==='jump')&&panel.hidden&&!transitioning)refreshHeldActions();else if(action==='interact')interact();else if(action==='pulse')attack();else if(action==='guard')pressGuard();else if(action==='save')persist();
     else if(action==='build')openPanel('build');else if(action==='journal')openPanel('journal');
    });
    addEventListener('keyup',e=>{keys.delete(e.code);if(e.code==='KeyC'||e.code==='Space')refreshHeldActions();});
    canvas.addEventListener('wheel',e=>{if(!panel.hidden)return;inputMode='desktop';zoom=THREE.MathUtils.clamp(zoom+e.deltaY*.025,17,58);e.preventDefault();},{passive:false});
    canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
     if(e.pointerType==='touch'){touchUI=true;inputMode='touch';updateTouchUI();}
     if(pointers.begin(e.pointerId,e.clientX,e.clientY,innerWidth,e.pointerType,e.button,panel.hidden&&physicsReady&&!transitioning)){
      canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);e.preventDefault();
     }
    });
    canvas.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{const result=pointers.move(e.pointerId,e.clientX,e.clientY);orbit+=result.orbit;zoom=THREE.MathUtils.clamp(zoom*result.zoomRatio,17,58);if(result.handled)e.preventDefault();});
    for(const event of ['pointerup','pointercancel','lostpointercapture'])canvas.addEventListener(event,e=>{const id=(e as PointerEvent).pointerId;pointers.end(id);if(event!=='lostpointercapture'&&canvas.hasPointerCapture(id))canvas.releasePointerCapture(id);});
    canvas.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>e.preventDefault());
    addEventListener('blur',()=>{pauseLivePractice('blur');windowActive=false;coop.setSuspended(true);clearInput();});addEventListener('focus',()=>{windowActive=true;coop.setSuspended(document.hidden);if(!panel.hidden&&panel.dataset.type==='dev')renderDevPanel();});addEventListener('pagehide',()=>{pauseLivePractice('hidden');windowActive=false;coop.setSuspended(true);clearInput();});addEventListener('pageshow',()=>{windowActive=document.hasFocus();coop.setSuspended(document.hidden||!windowActive);});addEventListener('resize',()=>{pauseLivePractice('resize');clearInput();});document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{coop.setSuspended(document.hidden||!windowActive);if(document.hidden){pauseLivePractice('hidden');clearInput();}});
    type FrontierTarget={id:string;kind:'workplace'|'supply'|'resident'|'cave-device'|'garden'|'wilderness'|'wilderness-spent'|'regional-outpost'|'regional-trade'|'regional-food';x:number;y:number;z:number;label:string};
    function nearest(){
     if(labActive)return undefined;
     let best:WorldObject|FrontierTarget|undefined;let distance=3.5;
     const candidates:(WorldObject|FrontierTarget)[]=[...objects];
     if(state.zone==='valley'&&state.frontierFood)for(const farm of regionalFoodPlan(state.seed).farms)if(regionalFoodTerrainConfirmed(farm.interactionPosition))candidates.push({id:farm.id,kind:'regional-food',...farm.interactionPosition,label:farm.name+' · food & meals'});
     if(state.zone==='valley'&&state.frontierTrade)for(const target of regionalTradeInteractions(state.seed,state.frontierTrade))if(regionalConfirmedTrade.has(target.targetId))candidates.push({id:target.targetId,kind:'regional-trade',...target.position,label:target.label.replace(/^Start |^Build /,'')});
     if(state.zone==='valley'&&state.frontierSupply)for(const outpost of regionalSupplyPlan(state.seed).outposts)if(regionalConfirmedProjects.has(outpost.id))candidates.push({id:outpost.id,kind:'regional-outpost',...outpost.deliveryPosition,label:outpost.name+' supplies'});
     if(state.zone==='valley'&&state.ecology&&gardenView)for(const plot of gardenView.plan.plots)candidates.push({id:plot.id,kind:'garden',...plot.position,label:plot.name});
     if(state.zone==='cave')for(const [id,p]of Object.entries(caveView.plan.anchors))candidates.push({id,kind:'cave-device',...p,y:0,label:id==='valve'?'Source valve':id==='pump'?'Drainage pump':'Sump drain · household outfall'});
     if(state.zone==='valley'&&frontierPlan&&state.causal){
      for(const place of frontierPlan.workplaces)candidates.push({id:place.id,kind:'workplace',...place.position,label:place.name});
      candidates.push({id:frontierPlan.source.nodeId,kind:'supply',...frontierPlan.source.position,label:'Waterworks loading point'});
      for(const resident of state.causal.agents){const identity=frontierPlan.agents.find(agent=>agent.id===resident.id);if(identity)candidates.push({id:resident.id,kind:'resident',...resident.position,label:identity.name});}
     }
     for(const candidate of candidates){if(candidate.kind==='rock'||state.collected.includes(candidate.id)||state.defeated.includes(candidate.id))continue;const d=Math.hypot(candidate.x-state.player.x,candidate.z-state.player.z,(candidate.y??0)-player.position.y);if(d<distance){distance=d;best=candidate;}}
     if(!best&&state.zone==='valley'){const feature=nearestGatherableWilderness(wildernessGatherContext(state,{feetY:targetPosition.y,obstacles:combatObstacles(),grounded:physicsMotion.grounded}));if(feature)return {id:feature.id,kind:'wilderness' as const,x:feature.x,y:feature.y,z:feature.z,label:feature.kind==='tree'?'Gather deadwood':feature.removable?'Pick up loose stone':'Gather stone chip'};const hand={x:targetPosition.x,y:targetPosition.y+.85,z:targetPosition.z};let spent:typeof wildernessCatalog[number]|undefined,reach=2.25;for(const f of state.regional?localWildernessCatalog():wildernessCatalog){if(!f.harvestable||!wildernessFeatureRemaining(f,state.wilderness)||!wildernessFeatureDepleted(f,state.wilderness)||Math.hypot(f.x-hand.x,f.z-hand.z)>4)continue;const p=nearestWildernessSolidPoint(f,hand);if(p.distance<reach&&wildernessSegmentClear(hand,p,combatObstacles().filter(o=>o.featureId!==f.id))){spent=f;reach=p.distance;}}if(spent)return {id:spent.id,kind:'wilderness-spent' as const,x:spent.x,y:spent.y,z:spent.z,label:spent.kind==='tree'?'Deadwood already gathered · trunk remains solid':'Stone chip already gathered · rock remains solid'};}
     return best;
    }
    function commit(next:State,action:AvatarAction=null){if(labActive)return;if(next===state){toast('You need more resources or a closer position');return;}state=next;if(action){animateAction(action);if(action==='gather'||action==='repair')gameAudio.cue(action);}sync();persist(false);const last=state.events.at(-1);if(last)toast(last);}
    function interact(){
     if(transitioning||!panel.hidden||contactRequestId!==null)return;
     const contact=contactUI();if(contact){if(combo.phase!=='idle'||guardBusy(guard)){toast('Finish the staff or guard action before gripping');return;}sendMovement();contactRequestId=`contact-${zoneEpoch}-${++contactRequestSequence}`;sendPhysics({type:'contact-grab',requestId:contactRequestId});return;}
     if(labActive)return;
     const object=nearest();
     if(!object){toast(state.zone==='dungeon'?'Get closer to a cache or the gold exit threshold':'Get closer to a resident, part, repair beacon, settlement flag or cave mouth');return;}
     if(object.kind==='wilderness-spent'){toast(object.label);return;}
     if(object.kind==='wilderness'){if(combo.phase!=='idle'||guardBusy(guard)||physicsMotion.crouched||!physicsMotion.grounded||contactBusy()){toast('Stand still and finish your current action to gather');return;}sendWorldAction({type:'gather-wilderness',id:object.id},'gather');return;}
     if(object.kind==='regional-food'){showRegionalFood(object.id);return;}
     if(object.kind==='regional-trade'){showRegionalTrade(object.id);return;}
     if(object.kind==='regional-outpost'){showRegionalSupply(object.id);return;}
     if(object.kind==='garden'){selectedGardenId=object.id;openPanel('ecology');return;}
     if(object.kind==='cave-device'){openPanel('cave-controls');return;}
     if(object.kind==='entrance'){openPanel('cave-mouth');return;}
     if(object.kind==='exit'){sendWorldAction({type:'exit'},'enter');return;}
     if(object.kind==='enemy'){attack();return;}
     if(object.kind==='workplace'){showFrontier({workplaceId:object.id});return;}
     if(object.kind==='resident'){showFrontier({agentId:object.id});return;}
     if(object.kind==='supply'){showFrontier({source:true});return;}
     if(object.kind==='pump'){if(state.waterRestored)toast(state.causal?'Original pump restored. Inspect the waterworks loading point and resident routes.':'Original pump restored. Your modular route supplies Mossbank’s live reserve.');else sendWorldAction({type:'repair'},'repair');}
     else if(object.kind==='settlement'){
      if(frontierPlan){const town=frontierPlan.settlements.find(item=>Math.hypot(item.position.x-object.x,item.position.z-object.z)<1);showFrontier(town?{settlementId:town.id}:{});return;}
      if(Math.hypot(object.x-endpoints.settlement.x,object.z-endpoints.settlement.z)>1){toast(object.label+': workshops and road connections are open. The waterworks commission board is at Mossbank.');return;}
      openPanel('settlement');
     }else sendWorldAction({type:'collect',id:object.id},'gather');
    }
    let combo=createComboState(),combatHeading=0,guard=createGuardState(),guardSnapshotAt=performance.now(),guardVisualCancelled=false;
    const guardView=createGuardView();scene.add(guardView.root);const enemyHP=new Map<string,number>();
    let impactHold=0,impactStrength=0,impactAge=0;const recoil=new Map<string,{x:number;z:number;age:number}>();
    function currentEquipment(){return equipmentFor(labActive?(labRunning?undefined:labEquipment):state.equipment?.active??undefined);}
    function combatTuning(){const base=equipmentCombat(currentEquipment());if(!labActive)return base;return sanitizeComboTuning({...base,inputBufferSeconds:labTuning.comboBuffer,staminaRegenPerSecond:labTuning.staminaRegen,attacks:base.attacks.map(a=>({...a,prep:a.prep*labTuning.comboTempo,active:a.active*labTuning.comboTempo,recovery:a.recovery*labTuning.comboTempo,knockback:a.knockback*labTuning.knockback}))});}
    function combatObstacles():Obstacle[]{return labActive?LAB_OBSTACLES:state.zone==='cave'?[...caveView.plan.walls,...(state.caveWater?caveFloodObstacles(state.caveWater):[])]:state.zone==='dungeon'?dungeon.walls:[...obstacles,...(contactEnabled()&&state.generation===2?[traversalPlan(state).ledge,...movableBodies]:[]),...machineWorldObstacles(state),...[...streamer.active.keys()].flatMap(k=>cellObstacles.get(k)??[]),...(state.regional?[...regionalConfirmedObstacles.values()].flat():[])];}
    function nearbyPulseTargets(){return labActive?labTargets.filter(t=>t.hp>0&&Math.hypot(t.x-targetPosition.x,t.z-targetPosition.z)<=3.5):pulseTargets(state,objects,combatObstacles());}
    function combatSnapshot(aim=false):ComboSnapshot{
     const canHit=physicsMotion.grounded&&!physicsMotion.crouched&&physicsMotion.stance<=0;
     const targets=labActive?labTargets.map(t=>({...t,alive:canHit&&t.hp>0})):objects.filter(o=>o.kind==='enemy').map(o=>({...o,alive:canHit&&!state.defeated.includes(o.id)}));
     let facing=combatHeading;
     if(aim&&!labRunning){const nearest=nearbyPulseTargets().slice().sort((a,b)=>Math.hypot(a.x-targetPosition.x,a.z-targetPosition.z)-Math.hypot(b.x-targetPosition.x,b.z-targetPosition.z))[0];if(nearest&&(inputMode==='touch'||Math.abs(Math.atan2(Math.sin(Math.atan2(nearest.x-targetPosition.x,nearest.z-targetPosition.z)-facing),Math.cos(Math.atan2(nearest.x-targetPosition.x,nearest.z-targetPosition.z)-facing)))<1.3))facing=Math.atan2(nearest.x-targetPosition.x,nearest.z-targetPosition.z);}
     return {player:{x:targetPosition.x,y:targetPosition.y,z:targetPosition.z,facing,hp:labActive?100:state.player.hp},targets,obstacles:combatObstacles()};
    }
    function consumeCombat(result:ComboResult){combo=result.state;let killed=false;
     for(const event of result.events){
      if(labRunning&&labMetrics){if(event.type==='stage')labMetrics.combat.stages.push(event.stage);if(event.type==='whiff')labMetrics.combat.whiffs++;if(event.type==='cancel'){if(event.reason==='stun')labMetrics.combat.stuns++;if(event.reason==='dodge')labMetrics.combat.cancelled++;}}
      if(event.type==='whiff')gameAudio.cue('staff-whiff');
      if(event.type==='stage'){gameAudio.cue('staff-prep',{seed:event.stage});avatarAction=null;combatHeading=combo.facing;}
      if(event.type!=='hit'||coop.active)continue;gameAudio.cue('staff-impact',{seed:event.stage});
      const current=labActive?labTargets.find(t=>t.id===event.targetId):undefined;
      if(labActive){if(current)current.hp=labInfiniteTargets&&!labRunning?100:Math.max(0,current.hp-event.damage);if(labMetrics){labMetrics.combat.hits++;labMetrics.combat.damage+=event.damage;labMetrics.combat.hp=current?.hp??0;}}
      else if(state.encounters){const wasDefeated=state.defeated.includes(event.targetId);state=commitEncounterHit(state,event.targetId,event.damage);killed=killed||!wasDefeated&&state.defeated.includes(event.targetId);}
      else{const hp=Math.max(0,(enemyHP.get(event.targetId)??100)-event.damage);enemyHP.set(event.targetId,hp);if(hp===0){const next=applyAction(state,{type:'attack',id:event.targetId});if(next!==state){state=next;killed=true;}}}
      impactHold=reducedMotion?0:Math.max(impactHold,event.hitstopSeconds*(labActive?labTuning.hitstopScale:1));impactStrength=event.stage/3;impactAge=0;
      if(impactHold>0)character.update({...animation,heading:combo.facing},null,0,{stage:event.stage,phase:'active',progress:.55});
      if(labActive||!state.encounters)recoil.set(event.targetId,{x:event.knockback.x,z:event.knockback.z,age:0});
      pulseTime=.25;pulse.position.set(event.position.x,event.position.y+.8,event.position.z);pulse.visible=true;
     }
     if(killed){sync();persist(false);toast('Sentry disabled. This route will remain clear');}
    }
    function wildernessImpact(id:string,point:{x:number;y:number;z:number}){wildernessView.hit(id);if(state.regional)regionalView?.hit(id);gameAudio.cue('staff-impact');pulseTime=.25;pulse.position.set(point.x,point.y,point.z);pulse.visible=true;impactStrength=.16;impactAge=0;}
    function attack(source:'manual'|'auto'='manual'){
     if(labActive&&livePractice){if(!coop.active&&!coopPending&&physicsReady&&!transitioning&&panel.hidden&&!document.hidden&&windowActive)sendLiveCommand({type:'attack',source});return;}
     if(guardBusy(guard)||coop.pendingGuardIntent)return;
     if(contactBusy()||!physicsReady||transitioning||!panel.hidden||document.hidden||!windowActive||(!labActive&&state.player.hp<=0)||physicsMotion.crouched||physicsMotion.stance>0||!physicsMotion.grounded)return;
     if(labRunning&&source==='manual')return;
     const result=requestComboAttack(combo,combatSnapshot(true),combatTuning(),source);if(coop.active&&result.events.some(e=>e.type==='stage'||e.type==='buffered')&&!coop.send({type:'attack-press'}))return;consumeCombat(result);
     if(source==='manual'){const rejected=result.events.find(e=>e.type==='rejected');if(rejected?.type==='rejected'&&rejected.reason==='stamina')toast('Staff recharging. Let the recovery finish');}
    }
    function stepCombat(){
     if(coop.active&&(physicsMotion.crouched||!physicsMotion.grounded)&&combo.phase!=='idle'&&coopCancelAttack!==combo.attackId){coop.send({type:'attack-cancel'});coopCancelAttack=combo.attackId;}
     if((physicsMotion.crouched||physicsMotion.stance>0||!physicsMotion.grounded)&&combo.phase!=='idle')consumeCombat(cancelCombo(combo,'dodge',combatTuning()));
     consumeCombat(stepCombo(combo,1/60,combatSnapshot(),combatTuning()));
     if(!labActive&&!coop.active&&physicsMotion.grounded&&!physicsMotion.crouched){const strike=resolveWildernessStrikes(combo,state,targetPosition,combatObstacles());combo=strike.state;for(const hit of strike.hits)wildernessImpact(hit.id,hit.point);}
     if(labActive&&labUnlimitedStamina&&!labRunning)combo={...combo,stamina:100};
     if(!labRunning&&(labActive?labAutoCombo:inputMode==='touch'&&mobileAutoPulse)&&comboEligibleTargets(combatSnapshot(true),combatTuning().attacks[combo.stage===1?1:combo.stage===2?2:0]).length&&(combo.phase==='idle'||comboBufferOpen(combo,combatTuning())))attack('auto');
    }
    function updateContextControls(){
     const threatened=labActive||objects.some(o=>o.kind==='enemy'&&!state.defeated.includes(o.id)&&Math.hypot(o.x-state.player.x,o.z-state.player.z,(o.y??0)-player.position.y)<5);
     document.documentElement.classList.toggle('combat-near',threatened);
     document.documentElement.classList.toggle('show-vitals',threatened||state.player.hp<100);
     document.documentElement.classList.toggle('movement-active',stick.pointerId!==null||crouch||physicsMotion.crouched||touchButtons.isPressed($('crouch-btn')));
     document.documentElement.classList.toggle('build-near',state.zone==='valley'&&GRID_CELLS.some(p=>Math.hypot(p.x+yardOrigin.x-state.player.x,p.z+yardOrigin.z-state.player.z)<=12));
     document.documentElement.classList.toggle('manual-touch-pulse',labActive||!mobileAutoPulse||inputMode!=='touch');
     const contact=contactUI();const object=nearest();const interactButton=$('interact-btn') as HTMLButtonElement;
     const label=contact?contact.label:!object?'Interact':object.kind==='wilderness-spent'?'Gathered':object.kind==='entrance'?'Enter caves':object.kind==='exit'?'Return':object.kind==='settlement'?object.label.replace(' settlement',''):['workplace','supply','resident','cave-device','garden','regional-outpost','regional-trade'].includes(object.kind)?'Inspect':object.kind==='pump'?(state.waterRestored?'Inspect':'Repair'):object.kind==='enemy'?'Inspect':'Gather';
     interactButton.disabled=contactRequestId!==null||!contact&&(!object||object.kind==='enemy'||object.kind==='wilderness-spent'||object.kind==='wilderness'&&(combo.phase!=='idle'||guardBusy(guard)||physicsMotion.crouched||!physicsMotion.grounded));interactButton.querySelector('.action-title')!.textContent=label;
     interactButton.querySelector('.action-hint')!.textContent=contact?contact.hint:object&&object.kind!=='enemy'?object.label:'nearby object';
     ($('attack-btn') as HTMLButtonElement).disabled=contactBusy()||guardBusy(guard)||!!coop.pendingGuardIntent||transitioning||physicsMotion.crouched||!physicsMotion.grounded||combo.phase==='stunned';
     ($('jump-btn') as HTMLButtonElement).disabled=coopPending||coop.active&&(!coop.canAct||!coop.snapshot?.motion);$('jump-btn').querySelector('.action-title')!.textContent=playerContact.mode==='hang'?'Climb':'Jump';$('jump-btn').querySelector('.action-hint')!.textContent=playerContact.mode==='hang'?'press to climb':coop.pendingJumpIntent?'confirming jump':coop.active?'server-confirmed jump':'press to jump';
     $('attack-btn').querySelector('.action-title')!.textContent=combo.stage?`Strike ${combo.stage} / 3`:'Attack';
     $('attack-btn').querySelector('.action-hint')!.textContent=combo.buffered?'next strike queued':comboBufferOpen(combo,combatTuning())?'tap to chain':combo.phase==='idle'?'1–2–3 staff combo':combo.phase;
     const displayGuard=guardForDisplay();($('guard-btn') as HTMLButtonElement).disabled=contactBusy()||labRunning||transitioning||!physicsReady||coopPending||coop.active&&(!coop.canAct||!coop.snapshot?.guard)||!physicsMotion.grounded||physicsMotion.crouched||physicsMotion.stance>0||crouch||jump||coopJumpHeld||guardBusy(guard)||guard.cooldownRemaining>1e-9||combo.phase!=='idle'||combo.cancelLockRemaining>1e-9||combo.stamina<DEFAULT_GUARD_RECIPE.staminaCost||!!coop.pendingGuardIntent;

    function drawMap(){
     if(labActive)return;
     map.clearRect(0,0,240,240);map.save();map.translate(120,120);const scale=state.generation===2?112/worldBound(state):2;
     const cached=mapBases.get(state.zone);if(cached)map.drawImage(cached,-120,-120);else{
     if(state.zone==='cave'){map.fillStyle='#627c7f';for(const t of caveView.plan.tiles)map.fillRect((t.x-1)*scale,(t.z-1)*scale,2*scale,2*scale);}
     else if(state.zone==='dungeon'){map.fillStyle='#627c7f';for(const t of dungeon.tiles)map.fillRect((t.x-1)*scale,(t.z-1)*scale,2*scale,2*scale);}
     else if(state.regional){drawRegionalOverview(map,scale);}
     else if(valleyPlan){
      const t=valleyPlan.terrain;for(let i=0;i<t.vertices.length;i+=3){const x=t.vertices[i]!,y=t.vertices[i+1]!,z=t.vertices[i+2]!;map.fillStyle=`hsl(130 18% ${28+y*1.6}%)`;map.fillRect(x*scale,z*scale,t.step*scale+1,t.step*scale+1);}
      function path(points:readonly {x:number;z:number}[],width:number,color:string){map.strokeStyle=color;map.lineWidth=width*scale;map.beginPath();points.forEach((p,i)=>i?map.lineTo(p.x*scale,p.z*scale):map.moveTo(p.x*scale,p.z*scale));map.stroke();}
      path(valleyPlan.river.points,valleyPlan.river.width,'#4c9f9c');for(const r of valleyPlan.roads)path(r.points,r.width,'#d7c597aa');
      map.strokeStyle='#edcf8d';for(const b of valleyPlan.buildings)map.strokeRect((b.origin.x-b.width/2)*scale,(b.origin.z-b.depth/2)*scale,b.width*scale,b.depth*scale);
     }else{map.fillStyle='#4c8f82';map.fillRect(-6,-120,12,240);map.strokeStyle='#d7c59788';map.lineWidth=4;map.beginPath();map.moveTo(-95,4);map.lineTo(100,4);map.stroke();map.strokeStyle='#edcf8d';map.strokeRect((workshop.origin.x-workshop.width/2)*2,(workshop.origin.z-workshop.depth/2)*2,workshop.width*2,workshop.depth*2);}
     const base=document.createElement('canvas');base.width=240;base.height=240;base.getContext('2d')!.drawImage($('minimap') as HTMLCanvasElement,0,0);mapBases.set(state.zone,base);}
     if(state.zone==='cave'&&state.caveWater)for(const b of caveView.plan.basins){map.fillStyle=caveDepth(state.caveWater,b.index)>.45?'#458cc6':'#71b4b1';for(const t of b.tiles)map.fillRect((t.x-1)*scale,(t.z-1)*scale,2*scale,2*scale);}
     for(const o of objects){if(state.collected.includes(o.id)||state.defeated.includes(o.id)||o.kind==='rock')continue;map.fillStyle=o.kind==='enemy'?'#cf947e':o.kind==='pump'?'#e9c17b':o.kind==='settlement'?'#eee4c0':'#a8d6b2';map.beginPath();map.arc(o.x*scale,o.z*scale,o.kind==='pump'?5:3,0,Math.PI*2);map.fill();}
     if(state.zone==='valley'&&state.ecology&&gardenView)for(const p of gardenView.plan.plots){map.fillStyle='#b4d888';map.fillRect(p.position.x*scale-2.5,p.position.z*scale-2.5,5,5);}
     if(state.zone==='valley')for(const p of GRID_CELLS){map.fillStyle=state.waterworks.parts.some(m=>m.x===p.x&&m.z===p.z)?'#83ebca':'#efce8680';map.fillRect((p.x+yardOrigin.x)*scale-1.5,(p.z+yardOrigin.z)*scale-1.5,3,3);}
     map.fillStyle='#fffbe1';map.beginPath();map.arc(state.player.x*scale,state.player.z*scale,4.5,0,Math.PI*2);map.fill();map.strokeStyle='#fffbe1';map.beginPath();map.arc(state.player.x*scale,state.player.z*scale,8,0,Math.PI*2);map.stroke();map.restore();if(state.regional&&state.zone==='valley'){map.fillStyle='#f3e9cb';map.font='10px sans-serif';map.fillText('10 km² · 3.162 km each side',8,13);map.strokeStyle='#f3e9cb';map.lineWidth=2;map.beginPath();map.moveTo(12,225);map.lineTo(12+500*scale,225);map.stroke();map.fillText('500 m',12,218);}const journalMap=document.getElementById('journal-map') as HTMLCanvasElement|null;journalMap?.getContext('2d')?.drawImage($('minimap') as HTMLCanvasElement,0,0);
    }

    function showSessionRecovery(message:string){
     let loading=document.getElementById('loading');if(!loading){loading=document.createElement('div');loading.id='loading';loading.className='loading';app.append(loading);}
     loading.innerHTML='<h1>AXIOM</h1><h2>Your saved world is protected</h2><p id="rescue-message"></p><button id="rescue-saves">Export local save recovery archive</button><button id="rescue-reload">Reload saved world</button><p id="rescue-status" role="status">Keep an archive before changing or clearing browser storage.</p>';$('rescue-message').textContent=message;
     $('rescue-saves').onclick=()=>{try{downloadJSON(localSaveArchive(localStorage),'axiom-local-save-recovery-archive.json');$('rescue-status').textContent='Recovery archive exported. It contains exact save bytes for repair, rather than a single-world import.';}catch{$('rescue-status').textContent='The browser still denies storage access. Do not clear site data; try allowing storage and reload.';}};$('rescue-reload').onclick=()=>location.reload();
    }
    /** Reject pre-reset documents, including a second tab restored from bfcache. */
    function ensureSessionRevision(){
     if(sessionReloading||sessionLoadFailed)return false;
     try{if(sessionResetRevision(localStorage,state)===persistedSessionRevision)return true;}catch{}
     sessionLoadFailed=true;clearInput();coopPanelDispose?.();coopPanelDispose=null;coop.disconnect();transitioning=true;physicsReady=false;zoneEpoch++;worker.onmessage=null;worker.terminate();renderer?.setAnimationLoop(null);mapPanelDispose?.();mapPanelDispose=null;generationPanelDispose?.();generationPanelDispose=null;regionalStreamer?.dispose();disposeGeneration();gameAudio.suspend();
     showSessionRecovery('This world was restarted or recovered in another tab, or its storage changed. Reload to use the current saved run. This older tab cannot overwrite it.');return false;
    }
    addEventListener('storage',event=>{if(!coop.active&&!coopPending&&(event.key===null||event.key===sessionEpochKey(state)))ensureSessionRevision();});
    addEventListener('pageshow',(event:PageTransitionEvent)=>{if(event.persisted&&!coop.active&&!coopPending&&!sessionReloading)ensureSessionRevision();});
    async function start(){if(sessionLoadFailed){showSessionRecovery('Local storage could not be read safely.     let frontierSelection:{settlementId?:string;workplaceId?:string;agentId?:string;source?:boolean}={};

    function renderFrontierPanel(){
     mountFrontierBoard(panel,state,{
      close:closePanel,journal:()=>openPanel('journal'),commissions:()=>openPanel('settlement'),barter:()=>openPanel('barter'),waterRequests:()=>openPanel('water-requests'),continuesInMenus:coop.active,
      ...(frontierSelection.settlementId?{selectedSettlementId:frontierSelection.settlementId}:{}),

      ...(frontierSelection.agentId?{selectedAgentId:frontierSelection.agentId}:{}),

      command:command=>runCausal(command),
     });
     const focus=frontierSelection.workplaceId?panel.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-workplace-id="${CSS.escape(frontierSelection.workplaceId)}"]`):frontierSelection.agentId?panel.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-agent-id="${CSS.escape(frontierSelection.agentId)}"]`):frontierSelection.source?panel.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.frontier-source'):null;

    }
    function runCausal(command:CausalCommand){
     if(labActive)return;
     if(!physicsReady||transitioning)return;
     const scroll=panel.scrollTop;
     sendWorldAction({type:'causal',command},command.type==='repair-workplace'||command.type==='repair-source'?'repair':command.type==='deliver-water'?'gather':null);
     renderFrontierPanel();panel.focus({preventScroll:true});panel.scrollTop=scroll;
    }
    
    function runBuild(command:BuildCommand){
     if(labActive)return;
     if(!physicsReady||transitioning)return;
     const result=build(state.waterworks,state.inventory,command,buildPlayer(state),state.zone);
     if(coop.active){sendWorldAction({type:'build',command});return;}const next=applyAction(state,{type:'build',command});toast(result.reason);
     if(next!==state){state=next;sync();persist(false);}renderBuildPanel();
    }
    let buildCell={x:-5,z:6};let buildKind:Part='pump';let buildRotation:Rotation=0;
    const directionLabel=(dx:number,dz:number)=>dx>0?'E':dx<0?'W':dz>0?'S':'N';
    const partSymbol:Record<Part,string>={pump:'P',pipe:'━',corner:'┏',elbow:'┗',tee:'┳',reservoir:'R',outlet:'O'};
    function renderBuildPanel(){
     const w=state.waterworks;const selected=w.parts.find(m=>m.x===buildCell.x&&m.z===buildCell.z);
     const preview:Module=selected??{id:'preview',kind:buildKind,...buildCell,rotation:buildRotation};
     const command:BuildCommand={type:'place',kind:buildKind,...buildCell,rotation:buildRotation};
     const check=build(w,state.inventory,command,buildPlayer(state),state.zone);
     const ports=portsFor(preview).map(p=>`${p.direction==='in'?'IN':'OUT'} ${directionLabel(p.dx,p.dz)}`).join(' · ');
     panel.innerHTML=`<button class="close" aria-label="Close panel">×</button><span class="eyebrow">Modular yard · 18 snapped cells</span><h2>Shape a water route</h2><button id="workbench-build">Inspect procedural plan</button><p>Yard coordinates are local to this foundation. Select a cell, choose a module and rotation, then Place. Blue ports take water in; gold ports send it out. Join touching ports below. The yard and movement pause while you plan.</p><p class="machine-status">${supplyWorking(w)?'SUPPLYING MOSSBANK':inletWorking(w)?'FILLING RESERVOIR':'NO ACTIVE INTAKE'} · ${w.stored.toFixed(1)} / ${capacity(w)} L<br>${w.delivered.toFixed(1)} L delivered · ${w.parts.length}/${BUILD_AREA.maxModules} modules<br>Pack: ${state.inventory.scrap} scrap · ${state.inventory.core} core</p><div class="yard-compass">N ↑ · Mossbank to the west · river to the east →</div><div class="yard-scroll"><div id="yard-grid" class="yard-grid" role="group" aria-label="Waterworks construction cells"></div></div><p id="selected-cell" class="selected-cell"></p><div id="part-controls"></div><h3>JOIN FLUID SOCKETS</h3><div id="socket-list"></div><details><summary>Sample route & construction rules</summary><p>A simple route costs 1 core + 3 scrap: Pump at (−5, 6), Straight at (−8, 6), Reservoir at (−11, 6), Outlet at (−14, 6), all rotation 0. Connect each pair. You can instead route around the yard with rotated corners and tees.</p><p>Pumps draw from the east bank (x −5); outlets serve Mossbank from the west side (x −14 or farther west). One pump, one reservoir and one outlet; at most 12 modules. Blocked cells protect paths and salvage access. Stand within 12 m, off the intended footprint.</p><p>Intake 2 L/s; one outlet delivers at most 1 L/s. Unconnected branches create no water. Rotating clears that module’s joins. Dismantling refunds once and drains tank water. Every layout change saves on this browser; use Settings → Export save for a portable copy.</p></details>`;
     for(const cell of GRID_CELLS){
      const module=w.parts.find(m=>m.x===cell.x&&m.z===cell.z),b=document.createElement('button');b.className='yard-cell';
      b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(cell.x===buildCell.x&&cell.z===buildCell.z));
      b.setAttribute('aria-label',`${cell.x}, ${cell.z}: ${module?PART_DEFS[module.kind].label+' rotation '+module.rotation*90+' degrees':'empty cell'}`);
      const symbol=document.createElement('strong');symbol.textContent=module?partSymbol[module.kind]:'·';if(module&&['pipe','corner','elbow','tee'].includes(module.kind))symbol.style.transform=`rotate(${module.rotation*90}deg)`;
      const coords=document.createElement('span');coords.textContent=`${cell.x},${cell.z}`;b.append(symbol,coords);
      if(module)b.classList.add('occupied');b.onclick=()=>{buildCell={...cell};renderBuildPanel();};$('yard-grid').append(b);
     }
     buildSelection.position.set(buildCell.x+yardOrigin.x,yardOrigin.y+.1,buildCell.z+yardOrigin.z);
     $('selected-cell').textContent=`(${buildCell.x}, ${buildCell.z}) · ${PART_DEFS[preview.kind].label} · ${preview.rotation*90}° · ${ports}`;
     const controls=$('part-controls');
     if(selected){
      const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='row';const rotate=document.createElement('button');rotate.textContent='Rotate 90°';
      const rotateCommand:BuildCommand={type:'rotate',id:selected.id,rotation:((selected.rotation+1)%4) as Rotation};rotate.disabled=build(w,state.inventory,rotateCommand,buildPlayer(state),state.zone).machine===w;rotate.onclick=()=>runBuild(rotateCommand);
      const remove=document.createElement('button');remove.textContent='Dismantle';remove.disabled=state.zone!=='valley'||Math.hypot(buildPlayer(state).x-selected.x,buildPlayer(state).z-selected.z)>12;
      remove.onclick=()=>{const confirm=document.createElement('button');confirm.textContent=selected.kind==='reservoir'?'Confirm drain + refund':'Confirm refund';confirm.onclick=()=>runBuild({type:'dismantle',id:selected.id});remove.replaceWith(confirm);};row.append(rotate,remove);controls.append(row);
     }else{
      const label=document.createElement('label');label.htmlFor='part-kind';label.textContent='Module';const select=document.createElement('select');select.id='part-kind';
      for(const [kind,def] of Object.entries(PART_DEFS)){const option=document.createElement('option');option.value=kind;option.textContent=`${def.label} · ${def.cost.scrap} scrap + ${def.cost.core} core`;select.append(option);}select.value=buildKind;select.onchange=()=>{buildKind=select.value as Part;renderBuildPanel();};
      const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='row';const rotate=document.createElement('button');rotate.textContent=`Rotate preview · ${buildRotation*90}°`;rotate.onclick=()=>{buildRotation=((buildRotation+1)%4) as Rotation;renderBuildPanel();};
      const place=document.createElement('button');place.textContent='Place module';place.disabled=check.machine===w;place.onclick=()=>runBuild(command);row.append(rotate,place);
      const reason=document.createElement('p');reason.className='build-reason';reason.textContent=check.machine===w?check.reason:'Clear footprint. Place to spend the displayed materials.';controls.append(label,select,row,reason);
     }
     const links=[...w.links,...possibleLinks(w)];
     if(!links.length){const hint=document.createElement('p');hint.textContent='No matching joins yet. Place adjacent modules with an OUT port touching an IN port; rotate to align them.';$('socket-list').append(hint);}
     for(const link of links){
      const connected=w.links.includes(link),edge=linkEndpoints(w,link);if(!edge)continue;
      const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='socket-button';b.textContent=`${connected?'Disconnect':'Connect'} ${PART_DEFS[edge.from.module.kind].label} (${edge.from.module.x},${edge.from.module.z}) → ${PART_DEFS[edge.to.module.kind].label} (${edge.to.module.x},${edge.to.module.z})`;
      const action:BuildCommand={type:connected?'disconnect':'connect',link};b.disabled=build(w,state.inventory,action,buildPlayer(state),state.zone).machine===w;b.onclick=()=>runBuild(action);$('socket-list').append(b);
     }
     panel.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('.close')!.onclick=closePanel;const inspectBuild=document.getElementById('workbench-build');if(inspectBuild)inspectBuild.onclick=()=>openPanel('workbench');
    }
    
    function jobProgress(){
     const a=state.jobs.active;if(!a)return 'No active commission';
     if(a.id==='reserve')return `${Math.min(12,state.settlement.reserve).toFixed(1)}/12 L reserve · ${Math.min(10,state.waterworks.stored).toFixed(1)}/10 L tank`;
     const delivered=Math.max(0,state.waterworks.delivered-a.deliveredAt);
     return `${Math.min(20,delivered).toFixed(1)}/20 L delivered${a.id==='service'?` · ${Math.min(30,Math.max(0,state.settlement.served-a.servedAt)).toFixed(0)}/30 s served`:''}`;
    }
    function renderSettlementPanel(){
     const near=state.zone==='valley'&&Math.hypot(state.player.x-endpoints.settlement.x,state.player.z-endpoints.settlement.z)<=3.5;
     panel.innerHTML=`<button class="close" aria-label="Close panel">×</button><span class="eyebrow">Mossbank · settlement board</span><h2>Water for a living town</h2>${state.causal?'<button id="board-frontier">Living frontier · people & needs</button>':''}<p class="machine-status">${settlementNeed(state.settlement,state.waterworks)}<br>${state.settlement.reserve.toFixed(1)} / ${RESERVE_CAPACITY} L local reserve · demand 0.5 L/s<br>${state.waterworks.stored.toFixed(1)} / 20 L waterworks tank</p><p>Water delivered by your modular outlet feeds the town. Its reserve lasts ${Math.floor(state.settlement.reserve/.5)} seconds without supply. Menus pause time; close this board to let water flow.</p><p><strong>${state.jobs.renown} renown · ${settlementTitle(state.jobs.renown)}</strong><br>One-time commissions unlock permanent titles. All three earn Mossbank Steward.</p>${near?'':'<p>Visit the gold flag at Mossbank (${endpoints.settlement.x.toFixed(0)}, ${endpoints.settlement.z.toFixed(0)}) to accept or claim commissions.</p>'}<div id="commission-list"></div><h3>ORIGINAL RELIEF REQUEST</h3><p>${state.waterRestored?'Completed and preserved. The live town reserve is separate and requires modular supply.':'Repair the original pump with 3 scrap + 1 core, or deliver 3 water canisters here. This historical request does not feed the new live reserve.'}</p><div class="row"><button id="legacy-request">${state.jobAccepted?'Request accepted':'Accept original request'}</button><button id="legacy-deliver">Deliver 3 canisters</button></div><button id="board-build">Open waterworks build menu</button>`;
     const frontierButton=document.getElementById('board-frontier');if(frontierButton)frontierButton.onclick=()=>showFrontier({});
     const list=$('commission-list');
     for(const [index,job] of JOBS.entries()){
      const done=state.jobs.completed.includes(job.id),active=state.jobs.active?.id===job.id,available=nextJob(state.jobs)?.id===job.id;
      const card=document.createElement('section');card.className='job-card';
