      const progress=document.createElement('p');progress.className='job-progress';progress.textContent=done?`Completed · ${job.reward} renown awarded`:active?jobProgress()+(!supplyWorking(state.waterworks)?' · restore live supply to claim':''):`Reward: ${job.reward} renown${available?'':' · complete the earlier commission'}`;
      const button=document.createElement('button');button.className='job-action';button.dataset.job=job.id;
      button.textContent=done?'Reward received':active?jobReady(state.jobs,state.settlement,state.waterworks)?'Complete & claim reward':'Commission in progress':available?'Accept commission':'Locked';
      button.disabled=done||!near||!available||(active&&!jobReady(state.jobs,state.settlement,state.waterworks));
      button.onclick=()=>{sendWorldAction(active?{type:'claim-commission'}:{type:'accept-commission',id:job.id as JobId});renderSettlementPanel();};
      card.append(h,p,progress,button);list.append(card);
     }
     ($('legacy-request') as HTMLButtonElement).disabled=!near||state.jobAccepted||state.waterRestored;
     $('legacy-request').onclick=()=>{sendWorldAction({type:'accept'});renderSettlementPanel();};
     ($('legacy-deliver') as HTMLButtonElement).disabled=!near||state.waterRestored||state.inventory.water<3;
     $('legacy-deliver').onclick=()=>{sendWorldAction({type:'deliver'});renderSettlementPanel();};
     $('board-build').onclick=()=>openPanel('build');panel.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('.close')!.onclick=closePanel;const inspectBuild=document.getElementById('workbench-build');if(inspectBuild)inspectBuild.onclick=()=>openPanel('workbench');
    }
    function renderCameraPanel(){
     panel.innerHTML=`<button class="close" aria-label="Close panel">×</button><span class="eyebrow">AXIOM · settings</span><h2>Camera & movement</h2><div class="row"><button id="orbit-left" aria-label="Orbit camera left">↶ Left</button><button id="orbit-right" aria-label="Orbit camera right">Right ↷</button></div><div class="row"><button id="zoom-in">+ Zoom in</button><button id="zoom-out">− Zoom out</button></div><p id="camera-distance"></p><div class="row"><button id="camera-reset">Reset camera</button><button id="camera-done">Back to world</button></div><div class="row"><button id="camera-back">Back to Settings</button></div><p>World time pauses here. Camera changes are visible behind this panel.</p><h3>MOVEMENT</h3><p>Drag the left half of the world. Thumb distance sets speed continuously: tiptoe, walk, jog, run, then full sprint. Lift to brake. Drag the right half to orbit. Pinch with two fingers that both start on the right world half to zoom. The left movement finger is never reassigned; it can stay down during camera gestures or button taps. After pinching, the remaining right finger can orbit. Wheel zoom works on desktop; these buttons work on every device.</p><p>Four action positions stay fixed: X interacts, Y attacks, B crouches and A jumps. Hold B/Crouch for a slow crawl or press it while running for a momentum slide. Release to rise when there is clearance. Jump is always in its own A slot and can be used with the movement thumb held down. Menus and backgrounding stop movement immediately.</p><p>Keyboard: WASD move, Shift sprint, hold C crouch/slide, Space jump, Q/R orbit, E interact, F attack, B build, J journal, M world map, K save, Escape close.</p>`;
     const update=()=>{$('camera-distance').textContent=`Camera distance: ${zoom.toFixed(0)} m`;($('zoom-in') as HTMLButtonElement).disabled=zoom<=17;($('zoom-out') as HTMLButtonElement).disabled=zoom>=58;};
     $('orbit-left').onclick=()=>{orbit+=Math.PI/8;};$('orbit-right').onclick=()=>{orbit-=Math.PI/8;};
     $('zoom-in').onclick=()=>{zoom=Math.max(17,zoom-4);update();};$('zoom-out').onclick=()=>{zoom=Math.min(58,zoom+4);update();};
     $('camera-reset').onclick=()=>{orbit=.6;zoom=36;update();};$('camera-done').onclick=closePanel;$('camera-back').onclick=()=>openPanel('settings');
     panel.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('.close')!.onclick=closePanel;const inspectBuild=document.getElementById('workbench-build');if(inspectBuild)inspectBuild.onclick=()=>openPanel('workbench');update();
    }
    
    let fullscreenPending=false,fullscreenError='';
    function updateFullscreenUI(){
     const button=document.getElementById('fullscreen-toggle') as HTMLButtonElement|null,status=document.getElementById('fullscreen-status');if(!button||!status)return;
     const active=!!document.fullscreenElement,supported=typeof document.documentElement.requestFullscreen==='function'&&document.fullscreenEnabled!==false;
     button.disabled=fullscreenPending||(!active&&!supported);button.textContent=fullscreenPending?'Changing display…':active?'Exit fullscreen':'Enter fullscreen';
     button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(active));status.textContent=fullscreenError||(active?'Fullscreen is active. You can exit here or use your browser’s exit control.':supported?'Ready. Tap Enter fullscreen to hide supported browser controls.':'Fullscreen is unavailable in this browser or embedded view. The game still uses the available screen.');
    }
    async function toggleFullscreen(){
     if(fullscreenPending)return;fullscreenPending=true;fullscreenError='';updateFullscreenUI();
     try{if(document.fullscreenElement)await document.exitFullscreen();else await document.documentElement.requestFullscreen({navigationUI:'hide'});}
     catch{fullscreenError='Your browser could not change fullscreen. You can keep playing, or try again from a supported browser tab.';}
     finally{fullscreenPending=false;updateFullscreenUI();}
    }
    document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange',()=>{fullscreenError='';clearInput();resize();updateFullscreenUI();});
    document.addEventListener('fullscreenerror',()=>{fullscreenError='Fullscreen was not allowed. The game is still available in this view.';fullscreenPending=false;updateFullscreenUI();});
    
    function renderWorldsPanel(){
     panel.innerHTML=`<button class="close" aria-label="Close panel">×</button><span class="eyebrow">Pinned worlds · separate saves</span><h2>Choose a frontier</h2><p>The streamed frontier covers 10 km²: a 3.162 × 3.162 km square, in metres. It extends the starting valley into linked meadows, pine highlands, river corridors, redstone uplands and trail shelters. Terrain, structures and wilderness arrive in 64 m chunks.</p><p>Opening a world saves this one and reloads the complete session. Old worlds retain their original terrain and IDs. Entering a seed you've used before resumes its progress.</p><button id="world-regional-current">Open 10 km² frontier · current seed</button><form id="world-seed-form"><label for="world-seed">New world seed</label><input id="world-seed" type="number" min="0" max="4294967295" step="1" inputmode="numeric" required><button type="submit">Generate / open world</button></form><h3>SAVED WORLDS</h3><div id="saved-worlds"></div><button id="worlds-back">Back to Settings</button>`;
     ($('world-seed') as HTMLInputElement).value=String((state.seed+1)>>>0);$('world-regional-current').onclick=()=>{try{switchSession(selectRegionalSeed(localStorage,state.seed));}catch{toast('Could not open the separate frontier save');}};
     ($('world-seed-form') as HTMLFormElement).onsubmit=e=>{e.preventDefault();const input=$('world-seed') as HTMLInputElement,seed=Number(input.value);if(!input.value.trim()||!Number.isInteger(seed)||seed<0||seed>4294967295){toast('Enter a whole seed from 0 to 4294967295');return;}try{switchSession(selectRegionalSeed(localStorage,seed));}catch{toast('Could not prepare that world. Export your current save before trying again.');}};
     try{for(const saved of savedWorlds(localStorage)){const button=document.createElement('button');button.textContent=`${saved.state.regional?'10 km² frontier':saved.state.generation===1?'Original valley':'Connected valley'} · seed ${saved.state.seed}${sameWorld(saved.state,state)?' · current':''}`;button.disabled=sameWorld(saved.state,state);button.onclick=()=>switchSession(saved.state);$('saved-worlds').append(button);}}catch{$('saved-worlds').textContent='Browser storage unavailable';}
     $('worlds-back').onclick=()=>openPanel('settings');panel.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('.close')!.onclick=closePanel;
    }
    
    // The laboratory swaps only the physics/render world. Campaign reducers and storage
    // stay locked until the captured campaign is restored; no synthetic rewards are issued.
    const labRoot=new THREE.Group();labRoot.name='isolated-developer-lab';labRoot.visible=false;scene.add(labRoot);
    box(labRoot,0,-.12,0,40,.24,36,'#35545d');
    const grid=new THREE.GridHelper(36,36,'#7baba9','#47676e');grid.position.y=.012;labRoot.add(grid);
    for(const o of LAB_OBSTACLES){const b=box(labRoot,o.x,o.y,o.z,o.hx*2,o.hy*2,o.hz*2,o.hy<1?'#cdb17b':'#718a98');if(o.hy<1){const material=(b.material as THREE.MeshStandardMaterial).clone();material.transparent=true;material.opacity=.62;b.material=material;}}
    for(const [name,x,z] of [['RUN / BRAKE',-7,-14.5],['TURN / REVERSE',-7,-9],['FREE JUMP',-10,2],['CEILING',0,0],['SLIDE → CRAWL',0,8],['STAFF COMBO',8,-8],['WALL TEST',8,11]] as const){makeLabel(name,x,z,labRoot);labRoot.children.at(-1)!.position.y=3.7;}
    const strikeTrails=DEFAULT_COMBO_TUNING.attacks.map((a,i)=>{const points=staffSectorPoints(a).map(p=>new THREE.Vector3(p.x,p.y,p.z));const geometry=new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(points),material=new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({color:i===2?'#ffddb0':'#aaf4d5',transparent:true,opacity:.6,depthWrite:false});const line=new THREE.Line(geometry,material);line.visible=false;scene.add(line);return line;});
    const labTargetVisuals=new Map<string,THREE.Group>();
    function resetLabTargets(){for(const g of labTargetVisuals.values()){g.removeFromParent();g.traverse(o=>{if(o instanceof THREE.Mesh)o.geometry.dispose();});}labTargetVisuals.clear();for(const target of labTargets){const g=new THREE.Group();g.position.set(target.x,0,target.z);labRoot.add(g);mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.14,.24,.7,6),'#8da6ad',g,0,.35,0);mesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(.65,1),'#d49274',g,0,1.12,0);box(g,0,1.12,-.59,.32,.13,.05,'#ffe3a4');const ring=mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(.75,.045,5,32),'#e8bd81',g,0,.03,0);ring.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;labTargetVisuals.set(target.id,g);}}
    function cancelLabEntry(){if(!labEntering)return;labEntering=false;labCaptureToken++;clearTimeout(captureTimer);}
    function requestLab(){if(sessionReloading||coop.active||coopPending||coop.status==='connecting')return;if(contactBusy()){toast('Release the crate or finish the ledge move before entering the lab');return;}if(labActive||labEntering||transitioning||!physicsReady)return;labEntering=true;clearInput();sendPhysics({type:'capture',requestId:++labCaptureToken});captureTimer=window.setTimeout(()=>{if(labEntering){cancelLabEntry();toast('The lab could not capture the paused world. Try again once movement is ready.');renderDevPanel();}},5000);renderDevPanel();}
    function beginLab(physics:unknown){if(!labEntering||labActive)return;clearTimeout(captureTimer);labEntering=false;campaignCheckpoint={state,bytes:serializeSave(state),physics,animation:{...animation},motion:{...physicsMotion},position:player.position.clone(),target:targetPosition.clone(),camera:cameraTarget.clone(),orbit,zoom,combo:structuredClone(combo),guard:structuredClone(guard),heading:combatHeading,hp:new Map(enemyHP),action:avatarAction,actionAge,actionDuration,saveStatus};labActive=true;document.documentElement.classList.add('dev-lab-active');$('lab-banner').hidden=false;valleyRoot.visible=false;dungeonRoot.visible=false;caveRoot.visible=false;labRoot.visible=true;scene.background=new THREE.Color('#233f4b');scene.fog=new THREE.Fog('#233f4b',55,100);resetLabCase();closePanel();focusGameplay();toast('Isolated lab open. Your campaign is paused and its saves are locked');}
    function resetLabCase(){playerContact=emptyPlayerContact();movableBodies=[];if(!labActive)return;stopLivePractice('suite');visualCapture.cancel('Course reset');clearInput();transitioning=true;physicsReady=false;zoneEpoch++;labTicks=0;lastLabStep=-1;labWarmup=60;labFrames=[];labMetrics=null;combo=createComboState(combatTuning());guard=createGuardState();avatarAction=null;impactHold=0;impactAge=1;recoil.clear();pulseTime=0;pulse.visible=false;
     const c=LAB_CASES[labCaseIndex]!;if(labCaptureOnly)visualCapture.arm({scenario:c.id,ticks:(LAB_CAPTURE_TICKS[c.id]??[]).filter(t=>t<Math.round(c.seconds*60)-3),maxTickLateness:2});labTargets=c.targets.map(t=>({...t}));resetLabTargets();combatHeading=c.facing;animation={...idleAnimation(),heading:c.facing};physicsMotion={vx:0,vz:0,vy:0,landing:0,landingSpeed:0,grounded:true,crouched:false,stance:0,sliding:false};targetPosition.set(c.spawn.x,0,c.spawn.z);player.position.copy(targetPosition);cameraTarget.copy(targetPosition);cameraTarget.y+=1;orbit=.5;zoom=23;sendPhysics({type:'zone',...c.spawn,y:0,bound:18,obstacles:LAB_OBSTACLES,sandbox:true});$('lab-live').textContent=c.name+' · '+(labRunning?`run ${labIteration}/${labIterations} · warming up`:'practice · saves locked');}
    function failLabRestore(reason:string){if(!pendingLabRestore)return;clearTimeout(restoreTimer);if(labReport)labReport.model.push({name:'Acknowledged physics pose restoration',pass:false,actual:reason,expected:'Matching post-restore worker capture'});pendingLabRestore=null;toast('Campaign data is safe, but movement could not be restored. Reload to recover your saved world.');}
    function exitLab(){if(!labActive||!campaignCheckpoint)return;interruptLab('Returned to campaign');clearInput();stopLivePractice('exit');const saved=campaignCheckpoint;const unchanged=serializeSave(state)===saved.bytes;state=saved.state;labActive=false;labRoot.visible=false;document.documentElement.classList.remove('dev-lab-active');$('lab-banner').hidden=true;transitioning=true;physicsReady=false;zoneEpoch++;combo=saved.combo;guard=saved.guard;combatHeading=saved.heading;enemyHP.clear();for(const [id,hp] of saved.hp)enemyHP.set(id,hp);animation=saved.animation;physicsMotion=saved.motion;avatarAction=saved.action;actionAge=saved.actionAge;actionDuration=saved.actionDuration;orbit=saved.orbit;zoom=saved.zoom;saveStatus=saved.saveStatus;impactHold=0;impactAge=1;pulse.visible=false;pulseTime=0;recoil.clear();for(const key of new Set([...residentGroups.keys(),...pendingCells.keys()]))disposeCell(key);streamer=new CellStreamer(streamObjects,cellPositions);machineSignature='';showZone();player.position.copy(saved.position);targetPosition.copy(saved.target);cameraTarget.copy(saved.camera);syncFrontierVisuals(0,true);
     pendingLabRestore={id:'lab-restore-'+zoneEpoch,physics:saved.physics};restoreTimer=window.setTimeout(()=>failLabRestore('Worker restoration acknowledgment timed out'),5000);if(state.regional)void sendWorldPacket('zone',{checkpoint:saved.physics});else sendPhysics({type:'zone',x:state.player.x,z:state.player.z,y:contactSpawnY(),obstacles:zoneObstacles(),...contactWorldPacket(),...initialCellPacket(),bound:worldBound(state),checkpoint:saved.physics,...(state.zone==='valley'&&valleyPlan?{terrain:valleyPlan.terrain}:{})});
     if(labReport)labReport.model.push({name:'Campaign state unchanged on exit',pass:unchanged&&serializeSave(state)===saved.bytes,actual:unchanged?'Campaign bytes unchanged; physics restoration requested':'Campaign snapshot restored after unexpected mutation',expected:'Inventory, seed, causal actors, world and progression retain exact captured bytes'});campaignCheckpoint=null;closePanel();focusGameplay();sync();toast('Returning to campaign. Saved progress is unchanged');}
    function labInput():LabInput{return labWarmup>0?{x:0,z:0}:LAB_CASES[labCaseIndex]!.input(labTicks);}
    function tickLab(sample:LabSample&{step?:number;playback?:{id:string;warmup:number;tick:number}}){if(livePractice){tickLivePractice(sample);return;}if(!labRunning){const result=stepGuard(guard,1/60,guardContext());guard=result.state;consumeGuardEvents(result.events);stepCombat();return;}if(!sample.playback||sample.playback.id!==LAB_CASES[labCaseIndex]!.id)return;if(sample.playback.warmup>0){labWarmup=sample.playback.warmup-1;if(labWarmup===0){labMetrics=newMetrics(sample);labFrames=[];$('lab-live').textContent=`${LAB_CASES[labCaseIndex]!.name} · run ${labIteration}/${labIterations}`;}sendMovement();return;}
     labWarmup=0;labTicks=sample.playback.tick;if(!labMetrics)labMetrics=newMetrics(sample);const command=LAB_CASES[labCaseIndex]!.input(labTicks);if(command.attack&&(combo.phase==='idle'||comboBufferOpen(combo,combatTuning())))consumeCombat(requestComboAttack(combo,combatSnapshot(),combatTuning(),'auto'));if(command.cancel)consumeCombat(cancelCombo(combo,'dodge',combatTuning()));if(command.stun)consumeCombat(cancelCombo(combo,'stun',combatTuning()));stepCombat();sampleMetrics(labMetrics,sample);labTicks++;
     if(labTicks>=Math.round(LAB_CASES[labCaseIndex]!.seconds*60)){const c=LAB_CASES[labCaseIndex]!;labReport!.runs.push({iteration:labIteration,caseId:c.id,name:c.name,checks:c.checks(labMetrics,labTuning),metrics:structuredClone(labMetrics),frames:frameSummary(labFrames)});if(labCaptureOnly){sendPhysics({type:'lab-stop'});labRunning=false;labCaptureOnly=false;visualCapture.cancel('Selected case ended');labReport!.status='completed';labReport!.finishedAt=new Date().toISOString();openPanel('dev');toast('Rendered capture ready. Missing frames are marked, and visual review is still yours');return;}labCaseIndex++;if(labCaseIndex>=LAB_CASES.length){labCaseIndex=0;labIteration++;}if(labIteration>labIterations){sendPhysics({type:'lab-stop'});labRunning=false;labReport!.status='completed';labReport!.finishedAt=new Date().toISOString();labReport!.model=modelChecks(state.seed);openPanel('dev');toast('Measured runs complete. Review checks and the separate visual checklist');return;}resetLabCase();return;}sendMovement();}
    function interruptLab(reason:string){pauseLivePractice(reason.includes('resize')||reason.includes('Viewport')?'resize':reason.includes('focus')?'blur':reason.includes('hidden')||reason.includes('background')?'hidden':'menu');visualCapture.cancel(reason);if(!labRunning)return;labCaptureOnly=false;sendPhysics({type:'lab-stop'});labRunning=false;if(labReport){labReport.status='interrupted';labReport.finishedAt=new Date().toISOString();labReport.interruption=reason;}if(labActive){sendPhysics({type:'input',x:0,z:0,paused:true});$('lab-live').textContent='Measurement interrupted · '+reason;}}
    function startLabSuite(captureCase?:number){if(!labActive||transitioning||!physicsReady)return;stopLivePractice('suite');labEquipment=undefined;labIterations=Number((document.getElementById('lab-iterations') as HTMLSelectElement|null)?.value??3);labIterations=Math.max(1,Math.min(5,labIterations));labUnlimitedStamina=false;labInfiniteTargets=false;if(captureCase!==undefined)labIterations=1;labReport=createReport(state.seed,labTuning,labIterations,{backend,equipment:{version:0,recipeHash:'survey-staff-v12',seed:null},physics:'Rapier 0.20.0 worker / fixed 60 Hz',three:'0.186.0',viewport:{width:innerWidth,height:innerHeight,pixelRatio:renderer.getPixelRatio()},presentation:preset,touchPoints:navigator.maxTouchPoints,reducedMotion,webgpuAvailable:'gpu' in navigator,performanceMeaning:'render callback intervals; not GPU timings; arena only, not full valley',phoneVerified:false});labRunning=true;labCaptureOnly=captureCase!==undefined;labCaseIndex=captureCase??0;labIteration=1;if(labCaptureOnly)labIterations=1;closePanel();resetLabCase();}
    function downloadJSON(value:unknown,name:string){const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([JSON.stringify(value,null,2)],{type:'application/json'})),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download=name;a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);}
    function downloadSaveText(raw:string,name:string){const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([raw],{type:'application/json'})),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download=name;a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);}
    function updateLabVisuals(dt:number){for(const [i,line] of strikeTrails.entries()){if(combo.attack&&combo.stage===i+1){const key=combo.attack.range+':'+combo.attack.arc;if(line.userData.shape!==key){line.geometry.dispose();line.geometry=new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(staffSectorPoints(combo.attack).map(p=>new THREE.Vector3(p.x,p.y,p.z)));line.userData.shape=key;}}line.visible=combo.phase==='active'&&combo.stage===i+1&&!physicsMotion.crouched&&physicsMotion.grounded;if(line.visible){line.position.copy(player.position);line.rotation.y=combo.facing;const progress=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,combo.phaseElapsed/(combo.attack?.active??1)));line.geometry.setDrawRange(0,33);line.material.opacity=reducedMotion?.35:.7*(1-progress*.5);}}if(!labActive)return;$('hp').style.width=(livePractice?.hp??100)+'%';if(livePractice){livePracticeVisual?.update(livePractice.encounters.enemies[0]!);$('lab-live').textContent=livePracticeText();return;}for(const t of labTargets){const g=labTargetVisuals.get(t.id);if(g){g.visible=t.hp>0;g.userData.hp=t.hp;const body=g.children[1] as THREE.Mesh;body.material=mat(t.hp>65?'#d49274':t.hp>30?'#ddba77':'#94dec7');}}if(!labRunning&&panel.hidden)$('lab-live').textContent=`${LAB_CASES[labCaseIndex]!.name} · ${labTargets.length?labTargets.map(t=>`${t.hp} HP`).join(' / '):animation.gait} · ${Math.hypot(physicsMotion.vx,physicsMotion.vz).toFixed(1)} m/s`;}
    function renderDevPanel(){

     panel.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('.close')!.onclick=closePanel;($('lab-iterations') as HTMLSelectElement).value=String(labIterations);($('lab-case') as HTMLSelectElement).value=String(labCaseIndex);const showLook=()=>{$('lab-look').textContent=LAB_CASES[Number(($('lab-case') as HTMLSelectElement).value)]!.lookFor;};showLook();$('lab-case').onchange=showLook;

     $('regional-supply-lab').onclick=()=>{($('regional-supply-lab') as HTMLButtonElement).disabled=true;$('regional-supply-lab-status').textContent='Running disposable settlement scenarios…';window.setTimeout(()=>{try{regionalSupplyReport=runRegionalSupplyLab(state.seed,1,{sourceRevision:livePracticeSourceRevision});}catch(error){toast(error instanceof Error?error.message:'Settlement scenario stopped');}if(!panel.hidden&&panel.dataset.type==='dev')renderDevPanel();},0);};$('regional-supply-export').onclick=()=>{if(regionalSupplyReport)downloadJSON(regionalSupplyReport,'axiom-regional-settlement-report.json');};if(regionalSupplyReport)$('regional-supply-lab-status').textContent=regionalSupplyReport.status+' · disposable model and persistence checks; rendered quality remains a manual review';
     $('wilderness-probes').textContent=wildernessProbeController?'Cancel collision probes':'Run isolated solid probes';$('wilderness-probes').onclick=()=>{if(wildernessProbeController){wildernessProbeController.abort();return;}const controller=new AbortController();wildernessProbeController=controller;const repeats=Number(($('lab-iterations') as HTMLSelectElement).value);$('wilderness-probes').textContent='Cancel collision probes';void runWildernessLab(state.seed,repeats,{sourceRevision:livePracticeSourceRevision,signal:controller.signal,progress:(done,total)=>{const status=document.getElementById('wilderness-probes-status');if(status)status.textContent=`${done}/${total} disposable tree / rock fixtures measured`;}}).then(report=>{wildernessReport=report;if(wildernessProbeController===controller)wildernessProbeController=null;if(!panel.hidden&&panel.dataset.type==='dev')renderDevPanel();});};$('wilderness-probes-export').onclick=()=>{if(wildernessReport)downloadJSON(wildernessReport,'axiom-wilderness-collision-report.json');};($('wilderness-probes-export') as HTMLButtonElement).disabled=!wildernessReport;$('wilderness-outlines').onclick=()=>{wildernessView.setDebug(!wildernessView.debugEnabled);regionalView?.setDebug(wildernessView.debugEnabled);$('wilderness-outlines').textContent=wildernessView.debugEnabled?'Hide wilderness solid outlines':'Show wilderness solid outlines';};if(wildernessReport)$('wilderness-probes-status').textContent=`${wildernessReport.status}: ${wildernessReport.runs.filter(r=>r.pass).length}/${wildernessReport.runs.length} real-worker probes passed${wildernessReport.error?' · '+wildernessReport.error:''}. Visual review remains separate.`;
     $('lab-systems').onclick=()=>{systemsReport=repeatedSystemsReport(state.seed,Number(($('lab-iterations') as HTMLSelectElement).value));renderDevPanel();};$('lab-systems-export').onclick=()=>{if(systemsReport)downloadJSON(systemsReport,'axiom-world-system-checks.json');};$('lab-systems-summary').textContent=systemsReport?`${systemsReport.runs.length} repeats · ${systemsReport.runs.flatMap(r=>r.checks).filter(c=>c.pass).length}/${systemsReport.runs.flatMap(r=>r.checks).length} model checks passed · visual and online review separate`:'No model runs yet';$('lab-equipment').onclick=()=>openPanel('equipment');$('lab-enter').onclick=labActive?exitLab:requestLab;$('lab-run').onclick=()=>startLabSuite();$('lab-capture-run').onclick=()=>startLabSuite(Number(($('lab-case') as HTMLSelectElement).value));$('lab-capture-png').onclick=()=>visualCapture.download('png');$('lab-capture-json').onclick=()=>visualCapture.download('json');const captured=visualCapture.report();$('capture-status').textContent=`${captured.status} · ${captured.entries.filter(e=>e.status==='captured').length} captured · ${captured.entries.filter(e=>e.status!=='captured').length} missing/suspect/failed · visual review not approved`; $('lab-view').onclick=()=>{closePanel();focusGameplay();};$('lab-practice').onclick=()=>{labCaseIndex=Number(($('lab-case') as HTMLSelectElement).value);resetLabCase();closePanel();focusGameplay();};
     $('lab-auto').onchange=e=>{labAutoCombo=(e.target as HTMLInputElement).checked;};$('lab-stamina').onchange=e=>{labUnlimitedStamina=(e.target as HTMLInputElement).checked;};$('lab-targets').onchange=e=>{labInfiniteTargets=(e.target as HTMLInputElement).checked;};$('lab-reduced').onchange=e=>{reducedMotion=(e.target as HTMLInputElement).checked;};
     for(const input of panel.querySelectorAll<HTMLInputElement>('[data-tuning]'))input.oninput=()=>{if(livePractice){toast('Stop live practice before changing tuning');return;}const key=input.dataset.tuning as TuningKey;labTuning=sanitizeTuning({...labTuning,[key]:Number(input.value)});$('lab-value-'+key).textContent=labTuning[key].toFixed(2);sendPhysics({type:'tuning',value:labTuning});};
     $('lab-defaults').onclick=()=>{if(livePractice){toast('Stop live practice before changing tuning');return;}labTuning={...DEFAULT_TUNING};if(labActive)sendPhysics({type:'tuning',value:labTuning});renderDevPanel();};$('lab-save-preset').onclick=()=>{try{localStorage.setItem('axiom-lab-preset-v1',JSON.stringify({version:1,tuning:labTuning}));toast('Lab preset saved on this device');}catch{toast('Preset storage unavailable');}};$('lab-load-preset').onclick=()=>{if(livePractice){toast('Stop live practice before changing tuning');return;}try{const raw=localStorage.getItem('axiom-lab-preset-v1');if(!raw){toast('No saved lab preset');return;}const value=JSON.parse(raw);if(value.version!==1)throw new Error();labTuning=sanitizeTuning(value.tuning);if(labActive)sendPhysics({type:'tuning',value:labTuning});renderDevPanel();toast('Saved lab preset loaded');}catch{toast('That lab preset could not be loaded');}};

     if(report){for(const run of report.runs){const el=document.createElement('details'),summary=document.createElement('summary');summary.textContent=`Run ${run.iteration} · ${run.name} · ${run.checks.every(c=>c.pass)?'PASS':'CHECK FAILED'}`;el.append(summary);const p=document.createElement('p');const f=run.frames;p.textContent=`${f.sampleCount} actual frame intervals · p50 ${f.p50Ms?.toFixed(1)??'n/a'} / p95 ${f.p95Ms?.toFixed(1)??'n/a'} / p99 ${f.p99Ms?.toFixed(1)??'n/a'} ms · ${f.over33ms} over 33.3 ms`;el.append(p);for(const c of run.checks){const line=document.createElement('p');line.className=c.pass?'lab-pass':'lab-fail';line.textContent=`${c.pass?'PASS':'FAIL'} · ${c.name}: ${c.actual}. Expect ${c.expected}`;el.append(line);}$('lab-results').append(el);}for(const c of report.model){const p=document.createElement('p');p.textContent=`${c.pass?'PASS':'FAIL'} · ${c.name} · ${c.actual}`;$('lab-results').append(p);}}
     for(const c of LAB_CASES){const details=document.createElement('details'),summary=document.createElement('summary'),p=document.createElement('p'),select=document.createElement('select');summary.textContent=c.name;p.textContent=c.lookFor;select.setAttribute('aria-label',c.name+' visual rating');for(const [value,text] of [['not-reviewed','Not reviewed'],['pass','Looks good on my device'],['needs-work','Needs work']]){const option=document.createElement('option');option.value=value!;option.textContent=text!;select.append(option);}select.value=report?.manual[c.id]??'not-reviewed';select.disabled=!report;select.onchange=()=>{if(labReport)labReport.manual[c.id]=select.value as 'pass'|'needs-work'|'not-reviewed';};details.append(summary,p,select);$('lab-manual').append(details);}
    }

    addEventListener('blur',()=>{cancelLabEntry();interruptLab('Window lost focus');});addEventListener('resize',()=>interruptLab('Viewport changed'));addEventListener('pagehide',()=>{cancelLabEntry();interruptLab('Page hidden');});document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden){cancelLabEntry();interruptLab('Tab backgrounded');}});
    
    /** Menus pause combat, so changing gear cannot erase an outstanding attack deadline. */
    function equipLoadout(seed:number|null):string {
     if(labActive&&livePractice)return 'Stop live practice before changing its latched equipment';
     if(transitioning||!physicsReady)return 'Wait for the world transition to finish';
     if(!canSwapEquipment(combo))return 'Finish the attack, stun or recovery before changing equipment';
     if(guardBusy(guard))return 'Finish the guard commitment before changing equipment';
     if(labRunning)return 'Finish or interrupt the measured run first';
     if(labActive){labEquipment=seed===null?undefined:compileEquipment(seed).ref;character.setEquipment(currentEquipment());return 'Isolated practice loadout changed. Campaign materials untouched';}
     if(state.zone!=='valley'||state.player.hp<=0)return 'Return alive to the valley to refit equipment';
     if(coop.active)return coop.send({type:'refit-equipment',seed})?'Refit request sent to the expedition authority':'Online room is paused';const next=applyAction(state,{type:'refit-equipment',seed});if(next===state)return 'Already equipped, or not enough salvage after recycling the current assembly';
     state=next;character.setEquipment(currentEquipment());sync();persist(false);return 'Equipment saved. The previous assembly’s materials were recycled exactly';
    }
    
    /** Custom authoring remains data-only; the world/room authority recompiles the recipe. */
    function assembleLoadout(recipe:EquipmentRecipeInput):string {
     if(labActive&&livePractice)return 'Stop live practice before changing its latched equipment';
     if(transitioning||!physicsReady)return 'Wait for the world transition to finish';
     if(!canSwapEquipment(combo)||guardBusy(guard))return 'Finish the staff / guard commitment before changing equipment';
     if(labRunning)return 'Finish or interrupt the measured run first';
     let plan;try{plan=compileCustomEquipment(recipe);}catch{return 'Edited recipe rejected by the compiler';}
     if(labActive){labEquipment=plan.ref;character.setEquipment(currentEquipment());return 'Edited practice loadout changed. Campaign materials untouched';}
     if(state.zone!=='valley'||state.player.hp<=0)return 'Return alive to the valley to refit equipment';
     if(coop.active)return coop.send({type:'assemble-equipment',recipe})?'Assembly request sent to the expedition authority':'Online room is paused';
     const next=applyAction(state,{type:'assemble-equipment',recipe});if(next===state)return 'Already equipped, or not enough salvage after recycling the current assembly';
     state=next;character.setEquipment(currentEquipment());sync();const saved=persist(false);return saved?'Edited equipment saved. The previous assembly’s materials were recycled exactly':'Equipped for this session, but saving failed. Export the current save from Settings; the earlier saved slot is retained';
    }
    
    function renderCaveMouth(){panel.innerHTML=`<button class="close" aria-label="Close cave choice">×</button><span class="eyebrow">BENEATH VERDANT REACH</span><h2>Two ways below</h2><button id="enter-vault">Echo Vault · sentries & caches</button><p>The original branching machine vault retains every discovery.</p><button id="enter-river-cave">Natural river cave · water & routes</button><p>A winding cavern with flooded narrows. Choose a source-valve repair, working pump or cleared drain to reach the far grotto. Two dry-bank return lines keep an escape open.</p>`;panel.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('.close')!.onclick=closePanel;for(const [id,type]of [['enter-vault','enter'],['enter-river-cave','enter-cave']] as const)$(id).onclick=()=>{closePanel();sendWorldAction({type},'enter');};}
    function renderCaveControls(){if(!state.caveWater||state.zone!=='cave'){closePanel();return;}const nearby=Object.entries(caveView.plan.anchors).filter(([,p])=>Math.hypot(p.x-state.player.x,p.z-state.player.z)<=3.5).sort((a,b)=>Math.hypot(a[1].x-state.player.x,a[1].z-state.player.z)-Math.hypot(b[1].x-state.player.x,b[1].z-state.player.z))[0]?.[0]??null;mountCavePanel(panel,{state:state.caveWater,continuesInMenus:coop.active,...(state.caveSupply?{supply:state.caveSupply,connect:()=>{sendWorldAction({type:'cave-supply',command:{type:'connect-outfall'}},'repair');renderCaveControls();}}:{}),inventory:state.inventory,near:nearby,act:(command:CaveWaterCommand)=>{sendWorldAction({type:'cave-water',command},'repair');renderCaveControls();},close:closePanel});}
    
    function sendWorldAction(action:CoopAction,animation:AvatarAction=null){if(sessionReloading||labActive)return;if(action.type==='regional-trade'&&!canRegionalTradeAction(action.command)){toast('Freight action unavailable at your current position');return;}if(action.type==='regional-supply'&&!canRegionalSupplyAction(action.command)){toast('Settlement action unavailable at your current position');return;}if(!coop.active&&action.type==='build'){const trial=applyAction(state,action as import('./world').Action);if(trial!==state&&movableBodies.some(b=>machineWorldObstacles(trial).some(o=>Math.abs(b.x-o.x)<b.hx+o.hx+.03&&Math.abs(b.z-o.z)<b.hz+o.hz+.03&&Math.abs(b.y-(o.y??o.hy))<b.hy+o.hy))){toast('Move the survey crate clear of the construction before building');return;}}if(coop.active){if(coop.send(action)){if(animation)animateAction(animation);toast('Expedition action sent');}else toast('Online room is paused or reconnecting');return;}const next=action.type==='gather-wilderness'?commitWildernessGather(state,action.id,{feetY:targetPosition.y,obstacles:combatObstacles(),grounded:physicsMotion.grounded}):applyAction(state,action as import('./world').Action);if(next!==state&&next.zone!==state.zone){closePanel();restoreWorld(next,true);if(animation)animateAction(animation);toast(next.events.at(-1)!);}else commit(next,animation);}
    function receiveCoopSnapshot(snapshot:CoopSnapshot){
     if(sessionReloading||sessionLoadFailed)return;
     if(!coopBoot&&!ensureSessionRevision())return;
     if(!coopBoot||coopBoot.roomId!==snapshot.roomId||!sameWorld(state,snapshot.world)){
      try{const solo=coopBoot?.solo??state;if(!coopBoot)storeSession(localStorage,solo,true,persistedSessionRevision);writeCoopBoot(sessionStorage,snapshot.roomId,snapshot.world,solo,coopBoot?.soloRevision??persistedSessionRevision);coopPending=true;clearInput();transitioning=true;location.reload();}catch{coopLeaving=true;coop.disconnect();coopLeaving=false;toast('Your solo checkpoint could not be saved safely. The online switch was stopped; export your solo save and try again.');}return;
     }
     const previousCombo=combo;const previousGuard=guard,previousState=state,first=!coopAccepted,zoneChanged=state.zone!==snapshot.world.zone;const distantRelocation=!!state.regional&&state.zone==='valley'&&regionalRelocationNeeded(regionalLoadTarget,targetPosition,snapshot.world.player);coopPending=false;coopAccepted=true;
     if(first||zoneChanged||distantRelocation){restoreWorld(snapshot.world);combo=structuredClone(snapshot.combo);}else{state=snapshot.world;objects=encounterObjects(state);combo=structuredClone(snapshot.combo);const error=Math.hypot(targetPosition.x-state.player.x,targetPosition.z-state.player.z);if(!snapshot.motion&&(error>3||!coop.canAct)){sendPhysics({type:'teleport',x:state.player.x,z:state.player.z,y:worldHeight(state,state.player.x,state.player.z)});targetPosition.set(state.player.x,worldHeight(state,state.player.x,state.player.z),state.player.z);}}
     if(!first&&!zoneChanged){for(const id of snapshot.combo.hitIds){if(snapshot.combo.attackId===previousCombo.attackId&&previousCombo.hitIds.includes(id))continue;const feature=localWildernessCatalog().find(f=>f.id===id);if(feature)wildernessImpact(id,{x:feature.x,y:feature.y+(feature.kind==='tree'?.85:0),z:feature.z});}}
     guard=snapshot.guard??createGuardState();if(first||guard.time!==previousGuard.time||guard.castId!==previousGuard.castId||guard.phase!==previousGuard.phase||guard.spent!==previousGuard.spent)guardSnapshotAt=performance.now();if(!first)consumeGuardEvents(guardSnapshotEvents(previousGuard,guard));
     if(snapshot.motion&&!coop.pendingJumpIntent&&!transitioning)sendPhysics({type:'online-motion',revision:snapshot.revision,x:snapshot.world.player.x,z:snapshot.world.player.z,motion:snapshot.motion});
     if(!first){if(snapshot.world.player.hp<previousState.player.hp){gameAudio.cue('hurt');impactAge=0;impactStrength=.45;}for(const enemy of snapshot.world.encounters?.enemies??[]){const old=previousState.encounters?.enemies.find(e=>e.id===enemy.id);if(!old||enemy.zone!==snapshot.world.zone)continue;const distance=Math.hypot(enemy.x-snapshot.world.player.x,enemy.z-snapshot.world.player.z);if(enemy.phase==='prepare'&&old.phase!=='prepare')gameAudio.cue('enemy-telegraph',{distance});if(enemy.hp<old.hp)gameAudio.cue('staff-impact',{distance});}}
     sync();syncCavePhysics();const banner=document.getElementById('online-banner');if(banner)banner.hidden=false;
     if(!panel.hidden){const scroll=panel.scrollTop;if(panel.dataset.type==='coop')coopPanelDispose?.refresh();else if(panel.dataset.type==='equipment')equipmentPanelDispose?.refresh();else if(panel.dataset.type==='regional-food')renderRegionalFoodPanel();else if(panel.dataset.type==='regional-supply')renderRegionalSupplyPanel();else if(panel.dataset.type==='regional-trade')renderRegionalTradePanel();else if(panel.dataset.type==='frontier')renderFrontierPanel();else if(panel.dataset.type==='cave-controls')renderCaveControls();else if(panel.dataset.type==='build')renderBuildPanel();else if(panel.dataset.type==='economy')renderEconomyPanel();else if(panel.dataset.type==='barter')renderBarterPanel();else if(panel.dataset.type==='water-requests')renderWaterRequestsPanel();else if(panel.dataset.type==='ecology')renderEcologyPanel();panel.scrollTop=scroll;}
     if(snapshot.world.player.hp<=0&&coop.canAct)coop.send({type:'respawn'});
     if(snapshot.notices.length)toast(snapshot.notices.at(-1)!);
    }
    function leaveCoopSession(){if(sessionReloading||sessionLoadFailed||coopLeaving)return;try{if(coopBoot)queueSoloReturn(sessionStorage,coopBoot.solo,coopBoot.soloRevision??0);coopPending=false;clearInput();transitioning=true;location.reload();}catch{toast('Your solo checkpoint is retained. Session storage prevented returning; reload to retry.');}}
    
    function syncRegionalObjective(){
     if(syncRegionalTradeObjective())return;
     if(!state.frontierSupply||state.zone!=='valley'||labActive)return;
     const inValley=Math.max(Math.abs(state.player.x),Math.abs(state.player.z))<100;
     if(inValley&&(state.jobAccepted||state.jobs.active))return;
     const plan=regionalSupplyPlan(state.seed),outpost=inValley?plan.outposts[0]!:plan.outposts.reduce((best,o)=>Math.hypot(o.position.x-state.player.x,o.position.z-state.player.z)<Math.hypot(best.position.x-state.player.x,best.position.z-state.player.z)?o:best),saved=state.frontierSupply.outposts.find(o=>o.id===outpost.id)!,available=regionalSupplyAvailable(state.frontierSupply,state.wilderness,state.frontierTrade);
     const missing={wood:outpost.cost.wood-saved.delivered.wood,stone:outpost.cost.stone-saved.delivered.stone};
     $('quest-title').textContent=outpost.name+' · '+(saved.builtAt!==null?'water for residents':saved.buildStartedAt!==null?'crew at work':'a lasting water reserve');
     $('quest-copy').textContent=saved.builtAt!==null?`${saved.consumed.toFixed(1)} L drunk by residents. Their finite tank collects modeled roof rain while the crew tends it.`:saved.buildStartedAt!==null?'The crew is assembling your delivered timber and stone. Close solo menus to let work continue.':`Gather ${missing.wood} wood and ${missing.stone} stone still needed. ${inValley?'Follow the west trail; M opens the full map.':'Look for the gold supply ring beside the shelter.'} X / E opens delivery and construction.`;
     $('quest-progress').textContent=saved.builtAt!==null?`${saved.water.toFixed(1)} / ${outpost.capacity} L STORED · JOURNAL FOR ALL COMMUNITIES`:`CARRY ${available.wood} WOOD · ${available.stone} STONE · ${Math.round(Math.hypot(outpost.deliveryPosition.x-state.player.x,outpost.deliveryPosition.z-state.player.z))} m TO SUPPLY POINT`;
    }
    function mountRegionalAtlas(){return mountWorldMapPanel(panel,{...(state.frontierTrade||state.frontierFood?{getOverlay:(current:State)=>{const freight=regionalTradeMapOverlay(current.frontierTrade),food=regionalFoodMapOverlay(current.frontierFood,current);return {markers:[...freight.markers,...food.markers],routes:[...freight.routes,...food.routes]};},inspectOverlay:(id:string)=>regionalFoodPlan(state.seed).farms.some(farm=>farm.id===id)?showRegionalFood(id):showRegionalTrade(id)}:{}),getState:()=>state,close:closePanel,back:()=>openPanel('settings'),continuesInMenus:coop.active||coopPending,...(state.frontierSupply?{markerDetail:(marker:import('./world-map').MapMarker,current:State)=>marker.kind==='outpost'?regionalSupplyMapDetail(current.seed,marker.id,current.frontierSupply,current.wilderness):undefined,inspectOutpost:showRegionalSupply}:{})});}
    function showRegionalSupply(id:string){if(!state.frontierSupply)return;selectedRegionalOutpost=id;if(!panel.hidden&&panel.dataset.type==='regional-supply'){renderRegionalSupplyPanel();return;}openPanel('regional-supply');}
    function canRegionalSupplyAction(command:RegionalSupplyCommand){
     if(!state.frontierSupply||!physicsReady||transitioning||labActive||sessionReloading||state.zone!=='valley'||state.player.hp<=0||coopPending||coop.active&&!coop.canAct||combo.phase!=='idle'||guardBusy(guard)||contactBusy()||!physicsMotion.grounded||physicsMotion.crouched)return false;
     const outpost=regionalSupplyPlan(state.seed).outposts.find(o=>o.id===command.outpostId);if(!outpost||!regionalConfirmedProjects.has(outpost.id))return false;
     const point=outpost.deliveryPosition;if(Math.hypot(state.player.x-point.x,targetPosition.y-point.y,state.player.z-point.z)>3.5)return false;
     const hand={x:state.player.x,y:targetPosition.y+.85,z:state.player.z};if(!wildernessSegmentClear(hand,{...point,y:point.y+.85},combatObstacles()))return false;
     if(command.type==='build'){
      const bodies=[{x:targetPosition.x,y:targetPosition.y+1.08,z:targetPosition.z,hx:.34,hy:1.08,hz:.34},...(movableBodies.length?movableBodies:traversalBodies(state))];
      if(regionalSupplyConstructionBoxes(state.seed,outpost.id).filter(b=>b.solid).some(box=>bodies.some(body=>Math.abs(body.x-box.center.x)<body.hx+box.half.x+.03&&Math.abs(body.z-box.center.z)<body.hz+box.half.z+.03&&Math.abs(body.y-box.center.y)<body.hy+box.half.y+.03)))return false;
     }
     return applyAction(state,{type:'regional-supply',command})!==state;
    }
    function renderRegionalSupplyPanel(){
     if(!state.frontierSupply){closePanel();return;}const id=selectedRegionalOutpost??regionalSupplyPlan(state.seed).outposts[0]!.id;
     mountRegionalSupplyPanel(panel,state.frontierSupply,{outpostId:id,...(state.frontierFood&&regionalFoodPlan(state.seed).farms.some(f=>f.siteId===id)?{food:()=>showRegionalFood(regionalFoodPlan(state.seed).farms.find(f=>f.siteId===id)!.id)}:{}),...(state.frontierTrade?{frontierTrade:state.frontierTrade}:{}),...(state.wilderness?{wilderness:state.wilderness}:{}),canRun:canRegionalSupplyAction,command:command=>{if(!canRegionalSupplyAction(command)){toast('Visit the supply point, stand clear and lower your staff before working');return;}sendWorldAction({type:'regional-supply',command},command.type==='build'?'repair':'gather');if(!coop.active)renderRegionalSupplyPanel();},close:closePanel,continuesInMenus:coop.active});
    }
    
    function renderEconomyPanel(){if(!state.economy||!state.causal){closePanel();return;}mountEconomyPanel(panel,{state:()=>({seed:state.seed,economy:state.economy!,causal:state.causal!,context:causalContext(state)}),act:command=>sendWorldAction({type:'economy',command},command.type==='repair-press'?'repair':'gather'),close:closePanel,...(frontierSelection.workplaceId?{selectedWorkplaceId:frontierSelection.workplaceId}:{})});}
    
    function renderEcologyPanel(){if(!state.ecology){closePanel();return;}mountEcologyPanel(panel,{state:()=>({ecology:state.ecology!,context:state}),act:command=>sendWorldAction({type:'ecology',command},command.type==='use-gel'?'repair':'gather'),close:closePanel,continuesInMenus:coop.active,...(selectedGardenId?{selectedPlotId:selectedGardenId}:{})});}
    
    function guardContext(){return {player:{x:targetPosition.x,y:targetPosition.y,z:targetPosition.z,facing:combatHeading,hp:labActive?(livePractice?.hp??100):state.player.hp,grounded:physicsMotion.grounded,crouched:physicsMotion.crouched||physicsMotion.stance>0||crouch},playing:(!labActive||!livePractice||livePractice.status==='active')&&physicsReady&&!transitioning&&panel.hidden&&!document.hidden&&windowActive&&!coopPending&&(!coop.active||coop.canAct),obstacles:combatObstacles()};}
    function consumeGuardEvents(events:readonly GuardEvent[]){for(const event of events){const cue=guardAudioCue(event);if(cue)gameAudio.cue(cue);if(event.type==='blocked'){impactAge=0;impactStrength=.2;toast('Guard intercepted one sentry strike');}}}
    function guardForDisplay(){return coop.active?confirmedGuardView(guard,(performance.now()-guardSnapshotAt)/1000):guard;}
    function cancelGuardInput(reason:GuardInterruptReason){if(labActive&&livePractice){if(reason==='jump'||reason==='crouch'){if(guardBusy(livePractice.guard)||livePractice.combo.phase!=='idle')sendLiveCommand({type:'cancel',reason});}else pauseLivePractice('menu');return;}if(coop.active){if(!guardVisualCancelled&&(guardBusy(guard)||coop.pendingGuardIntent))coop.send({type:'guard-cancel'});guardVisualCancelled=true;return;}const result=interruptGuard(guard,reason);guard=result.state;consumeGuardEvents(result.events);}
    function pressGuard(){
     if(labActive&&livePractice){if(!coop.active&&!coopPending&&guardContext().playing&&!crouch&&!jump)sendLiveCommand({type:'guard',intentId:crypto.randomUUID()});return;}
     if(contactBusy()||labRunning||!guardContext().playing||crouch||jump||coopJumpHeld)return;
     if(coop.active){if(guardBusy(guard)||coop.pendingGuardIntent)return;coop.move({x:targetPosition.x,z:targetPosition.z,y:targetPosition.y,facing:combatHeading,grounded:physicsMotion.grounded,crouched:physicsMotion.crouched});if(coop.guard()){guardVisualCancelled=false;toast('Guard requested · server timing includes latency');}return;}
     const result=requestGuard(guard,combo,guardContext(),crypto.randomUUID(),undefined,combatTuning());guard=result.state;combo=result.combo;consumeGuardEvents(result.events);const rejected=result.events.find(e=>e.type==='rejected');if(rejected?.type==='rejected')toast('Guard unavailable: '+rejected.reason);
    }
    
    function renderBarterPanel(){
     if(!state.causal?.commonsTrade){closePanel();return;}
     if(!panel.querySelector('#barter-body'))panel.innerHTML='<button class="close" aria-label="Close household barter">×</button><span class="eyebrow">People · shared stock · consequences</span><h2>Household barter</h2><p id="barter-clock"></p><div id="barter-body"></div>';
     panel.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('.close')!.onclick=closePanel;
     $('barter-clock').textContent=coop.active?'The online world keeps moving. Offers follow the latest authoritative household demand and stock.':'World time pauses while this board is open. Visit a household’s gold flag to exchange supplies.';
     mountCommonsTradeSection($('barter-body'),{state:()=>({causal:state.causal!,context:causalContext(state)}),act:command=>sendWorldAction({type:'commons-trade',command},'gather')});
    }

     if(!state.causal?.waterRequests){closePanel();return;}
     if(!panel.querySelector('#water-requests-body'))panel.innerHTML='<button class="close" aria-label="Close recurring requests">×</button><span class="eyebrow">Relief · consumption · fresh needs</span><h2>Recurring water relief</h2><div class="row"><button id="requests-deliver">Households & delivery</button><button id="requests-barter">Household barter</button></div><div id="water-requests-body"></div>';

    function livePracticeText(){if(!livePractice)return 'Live sentry practice is off';const p=livePractice,e=p.encounters.enemies[0]!;return `LIVE SENTRY · ${p.status}${p.pause?' ('+p.pause+')':''} · suit ${p.hp.toFixed(0)} HP · sentry ${e.hp.toFixed(0)} HP · ${e.phase} · guard ${p.guard.phase} · stamina ${p.combo.stamina.toFixed(0)} · seed ${p.seed} · tick ${p.tick}${p.status==='dead'||p.status==='defeated'?' · Reset for another attempt':''}`;}
    function presentLivePractice(result:LivePracticeResult){
     if(!labActive||!livePractice||result.state.epoch!==livePractice.epoch||result.state.epoch!==zoneEpoch)return;
     livePractice=result.state;guard=structuredClone(livePractice.guard);consumeGuardEvents(result.guardEvents);
     // Static targets are absent in this mode. This adapter is presentation only; the controller already committed its hit.
     consumeCombat({state:structuredClone(livePractice.combo),events:result.comboEvents});
     for(const event of result.encounterEvents){if(event.type==='prepare')gameAudio.cue('enemy-telegraph');if(event.type==='damage'){sendPhysics({type:'contact-release'});gameAudio.cue('hurt');impactAge=0;impactStrength=.45;}}
     for(const [kind,events] of [['combo',result.comboEvents],['guard',result.guardEvents],['encounter',result.encounterEvents],['contact',result.contacts]] as const)for(const detail of events)if(livePracticeEvents.length<512)livePracticeEvents.push({tick:livePractice.tick,kind,detail:structuredClone(detail)});
     if(result.reason==='snapshot-gap'||result.reason==='invalid-frame'){liveResumeRequested=false;visualCapture.cancel('Live practice interrupted: '+result.reason);toast('Live practice paused. Open Tune / report to Resume or Reset.');}
     if(livePractice.status==='dead'||livePractice.status==='defeated'){liveResumeRequested=false;visualCapture.cancel('Live practice ended: reset required');}
    }
    function pauseLivePractice(reason:LivePracticePause){if(!labActive||!livePractice)return;liveResumeRequested=false;if(livePractice.status==='active')sendLiveCommand({type:'suspend',reason});visualCapture.cancel('Live practice paused: '+reason);}
    function stopLivePractice(reason:'suite'|'exit'){if(!livePractice)return;if(liveRecording){const next=appendLiveSentryTrace(liveRecording,{type:'disable',reason});liveRecording=next;livePractice=next.state;}liveResumeRequested=false;visualCapture.cancel('Live practice stopped');livePractice=null;if(livePracticeVisual)livePracticeVisual.root.visible=false;}
    function startLivePractice(){
     if(!labActive||coop.active||coopPending||transitioning||!physicsReady)return;
     interruptLab('Live practice selected');clearInput();transitioning=true;physicsReady=false;zoneEpoch++;lastLabStep=-1;labWarmup=0;labTicks=0;labFrames=[];labMetrics=null;labCaptureOnly=false;labUnlimitedStamina=false;labInfiniteTargets=false;labAutoCombo=false;labTargets=[];resetLabTargets();
     liveRecording=createLiveSentryTrace({seed:state.seed,epoch:zoneEpoch,tuning:combatTuning()});livePractice=liveRecording.state;liveResumeRequested=true;livePracticeEvents=[];combo=structuredClone(livePractice.combo);guard=structuredClone(livePractice.guard);avatarAction=null;impactHold=0;impactAge=1;recoil.clear();pulseTime=0;pulse.visible=false;
     livePracticeVisual?.dispose();livePracticeVisual=createQuadruped(livePractice.bodySeed);labRoot.add(livePracticeVisual.root);livePracticeVisual.update(livePractice.encounters.enemies[0]!);
     const p=LIVE_SENTRY_ARENA.player;combatHeading=p.facing;animation={...idleAnimation(),heading:p.facing};physicsMotion={vx:0,vz:0,vy:0,landing:0,landingSpeed:0,grounded:true,crouched:false,stance:0,sliding:false};targetPosition.set(p.x,p.y,p.z);player.position.copy(targetPosition);cameraTarget.copy(targetPosition);cameraTarget.y+=1;orbit=.5;zoom=23;
     sendPhysics({type:'zone',x:p.x,y:p.y,z:p.z,bound:LIVE_SENTRY_ARENA.bound,obstacles:LAB_OBSTACLES,sandbox:true});closePanel();focusGameplay();liveResumeRequested=true;toast('Live practice: read the warning, move or time G. Campaign saves remain locked.');
    }
    function tickLivePractice(sample:LabSample&{step?:number}){
     if(!livePractice)return;if(coop.active||coopPending){stopLivePractice('exit');return;}
     const f:LivePracticeFrame={epoch:zoneEpoch,step:sample.step??-1,x:sample.x,z:sample.z,feetY:sample.feetY,facing:combatHeading,grounded:sample.grounded,crouched:sample.crouched,stance:sample.stance};
     if(livePractice.status==='ready'||livePractice.status==='paused'){if(liveResumeRequested){liveResumeRequested=false;sendLiveCommand({type:'resume',frame:f});}return;}
     sendLiveCommand({type:'step',frame:f});
    }
    function captureLivePractice(){if(!labActive||!livePractice||!['active','paused','ready'].includes(livePractice.status))return;closePanel();focusGameplay();liveResumeRequested=true;visualCapture.arm({scenario:LIVE_SENTRY_CASE_ID,ticks:[1,12,24,36,48,60,72,84,96,108,120].map(t=>livePractice!.tick+t),maxTickLateness:2});}
    function exportLivePractice(){if(!liveRecording)return;try{const trace=exportLiveSentryTrace(liveRecording);downloadJSON({sourceRevision:livePracticeSourceRevision,trace,currentSnapshot:liveSentrySnapshot(liveRecording.state),eventCounts:{staffHits:livePracticeEvents.filter(e=>e.kind==='combo'&&(e.detail as {type?:string}).type==='hit').length,guardBlocks:livePracticeEvents.filter(e=>e.kind==='guard'&&(e.detail as {type?:string}).type==='blocked').length,contacts:livePracticeEvents.filter(e=>e.kind==='contact').length},eventCountsCapped:livePracticeEvents.length===512},'axiom-live-sentry-trace.json');}catch{toast('The practice trace could not be exported. Reset for a shorter fresh attempt.');}}
    function sendLiveCommand(command:LivePracticeCommand){if(!labActive||!livePractice||!liveRecording||livePractice.epoch!==zoneEpoch||liveRecording.state.epoch!==zoneEpoch)return;const next=appendLiveSentryTrace(liveRecording,command);liveRecording=next;presentLivePractice(next.result);if((command.type==='attack'||command.type==='guard')&&!next.result.accepted)toast('Practice '+command.type+' unavailable: '+(next.result.reason??'recovery'));}
    
    /** Exact physical receipts for each target are owned by its existing terrain cell. */
    function regionalTradeChunkTargets(key:string):string[]{
     const plan=regionalTradePlan(state.seed),ids=[...plan.sources,...plan.projects].filter(target=>target.ownerChunk===key).map(target=>target.id);
     for(const route of plan.routes){const p=route.obstruction.interactionPosition;if(`${Math.floor(p.x/64)}:${Math.floor(p.z/64)}`===key)ids.push(route.id,route.obstruction.id);}
     return ids;
    }
    function showRegionalTrade(targetId:string){
     if(!state.frontierTrade)return;selectedRegionalTradeTarget=targetId;
     if(!panel.hidden&&panel.dataset.type==='regional-trade'){renderRegionalTradePanel();return;}openPanel('regional-trade');
    }
    function canRegionalTradeAction(command:RegionalTradeCommand):boolean {
     if(!state.frontierTrade||!physicsReady||transitioning||labActive||sessionReloading||state.zone!=='valley'||state.player.hp<=0||coopPending||coop.active&&!coop.canAct||combo.phase!=='idle'||guardBusy(guard)||contactBusy()||!physicsMotion.grounded||physicsMotion.crouched)return false;
     if(regionalConfirmedTrade.get(command.targetId)!==regionalTradeTargetSignature(state.seed,state.frontierTrade,command.targetId))return false;
     const point=regionalTradeCommandPosition(state.seed,command);if(!point||!regionalConfirmedObstacles.has(`region:${Math.floor(point.x/64)}:${Math.floor(point.z/64)}`)||Math.abs(targetPosition.y-point.y)>.45||Math.hypot(state.player.x-point.x,state.player.z-point.z)>3)return false;
     const hand={x:state.player.x,y:targetPosition.y+.85,z:state.player.z};if(!wildernessSegmentClear(hand,{...point,y:point.y+.85},combatObstacles()))return false;
     if(command.type==='start-source'||command.type==='build-store'){
      const bodies=[{x:targetPosition.x,y:targetPosition.y+1.08,z:targetPosition.z,hx:.34,hy:1.08,hz:.34},...(movableBodies.length?movableBodies:traversalBodies(state))];
      if(regionalTradeConstructionBoxes(state.seed,command.targetId).filter(b=>b.solid).some(box=>bodies.some(body=>Math.abs(body.x-box.center.x)<body.hx+box.half.x+.03&&Math.abs(body.z-box.center.z)<body.hz+box.half.z+.03&&Math.abs(body.y-box.center.y)<body.hy+box.half.y+.03)))return false;
     }
     return applyAction(state,{type:'regional-trade',command})!==state;
    }
    function renderRegionalTradePanel(){
     if(!state.frontierTrade){closePanel();return;}
     const targetId=selectedRegionalTradeTarget??regionalTradePlan(state.seed).sources[0]!.id;
     mountRegionalTradePanel(panel,state.frontierTrade,{targetId,...(state.frontierFood?{food:()=>{const plan=regionalTradePlan(state.seed),route=plan.routes.find(r=>[r.id,r.sourceId,r.projectId,r.obstruction.id].includes(targetId)),farm=regionalFoodPlan(state.seed).farms.find(f=>f.storeId===route?.projectId);if(farm)showRegionalFood(farm.id);}}:{}),readState:()=>state.frontierTrade,canRun:canRegionalTradeAction,command:command=>{if(!canRegionalTradeAction(command)){toast('Visit the freight work point and stand clear before starting');return;}sendWorldAction({type:'regional-trade',command},command.type==='start-source'||command.type==='withdraw-reserve'?'gather':'repair');if(!coop.active)renderRegionalTradePanel();},close:closePanel,map:()=>openPanel('map'),continuesInMenus:coop.active});
    }
    
    function syncRegionalTradeObjective():boolean {
     if(!state.frontierTrade||state.zone!=='valley'||labActive)return false;
     const active=state.frontierTrade.routes.some(route=>route.sourceStartedAt!==null);
     if(!active&&state.frontierSupply?.outposts[0]?.builtAt==null)return false;
     if(Math.max(Math.abs(state.player.x),Math.abs(state.player.z))<100&&(state.jobAccepted||state.jobs.active))return false;
     const plan=regionalTradePlan(state.seed),route=plan.routes.find((_,i)=>state.frontierTrade!.routes[i]!.sourceStartedAt!==null&&state.frontierTrade!.routes[i]!.withdrawn<8)??plan.routes.find((_,i)=>state.frontierTrade!.routes[i]!.sourceStartedAt===null)??plan.routes[0]!,summary=regionalTradeSummary(state.frontierTrade,state.seed,route.id)!;
     const source=plan.sources.find(s=>s.id===route.sourceId)!,project=plan.projects.find(p=>p.id===route.projectId)!,point=summary.phase==='planned'?source.interactionPosition:summary.phase==='blocked'||summary.phase==='repairing'?route.obstruction.interactionPosition:project.interactionPosition;
     $('quest-title').textContent=summary.jobTitle;
     $('quest-copy').textContent=summary.cause+' M shows resource sites, cargo and freight roads. E / X inspects the local work point.';
     $('quest-progress').textContent=`${summary.cargo} CARGO · ${summary.destinationStock} STORED · ${summary.withdrawn} TAKEN FOR BUILDING · ${Math.round(Math.hypot(point.x-state.player.x,point.z-state.player.z))} m TO WORK POINT`;
     return true;
    }
    
    /** Food projections and local actions require the exact acknowledged, visible terrain cell. */
    function regionalFoodTerrainConfirmed(position:{x:number;z:number}):boolean {
     const key=`region:${Math.floor(position.x/64)}:${Math.floor(position.z/64)}`;
     return regionalConfirmedObstacles.has(key)&&regionalView?.has(key)===true;
    }

     if(!state.frontierFood)return;selectedRegionalFoodFarm=farmId;
     if(!panel.hidden&&panel.dataset.type==='regional-food'){renderRegionalFoodPanel();return;}openPanel('regional-food');
    }
    function canRegionalFoodAction(command:RegionalFoodCommand):boolean {
     if(!state.frontierFood||!physicsReady||transitioning||labActive||sessionReloading||state.zone!=='valley'||state.player.hp<=0||coopPending||coop.active&&!coop.canAct||combo.phase!=='idle'||guardBusy(guard)||contactBusy()||!physicsMotion.grounded||physicsMotion.crouched)return false;
     const point=regionalFoodCommandPosition(state.seed,command);if(!point||!regionalFoodTerrainConfirmed(point)||Math.abs(targetPosition.y-point.y)>.45||Math.hypot(state.player.x-point.x,state.player.z-point.z)>3)return false;
     const hand={x:state.player.x,y:targetPosition.y+.85,z:state.player.z};if(!wildernessSegmentClear(hand,{...point,y:point.y+.85},combatObstacles()))return false;
     return applyAction(state,{type:'regional-food',command})!==state;
    }
    function renderRegionalFoodPanel(){

    }
